Sherlock Moriarty? He repeated the name in his mind.

Klein wasn't in a rush to find an opportunity to engage in true acting. He toured Oravi Island's port city with the mindset of a tourist, finding a brief and rare moment of relaxation in his tense life.

The city was populated mostly by immigrants from Loen. Its cuisine wasn't much different from that of the kingdom's east coast, except for the rare fruits and varied seafood that gave it a distinctive character.

Oravi was rich in natural resources and occupied a key position along the safe sea routes. The standard of living was fairly high. Even farmers in the suburbs could save money from their orchards.

This didn't mean Oravi had no poor or lower-class residents. That class consisted mainly of former slaves, as Loen's parliament had abolished slavery long ago.

After eating a sweet, juicy fruit, Klein watched the sky darken. He rounded a street corner and entered a bar named Sweet Lemon.

It was a famous gathering place for adventurers in Oravi. Klein planned to buy the two remaining supplementary ingredients for the Marionettist potion--the relatively common drago tree bark and Sonia's Golden Spring water.

The bar was quite lively. Many people raised their cups around the boxing ring and cheered loudly. At the surrounding tables, patrons who looked like adventurers discussed all kinds of rumors in hushed tones.

Just as Klein was squeezing towards the bar counter, he suddenly heard his name.

"I'm Gehrman Sparrow. You should know who I am. I received a treasure map and need to hire some helpers. It's not because I'm afraid, but because I can't carry that much treasure alone..." In a corner, a green-eyed man in his thirties held a half-full glass of liquor and spoke to two men and two women whose status as merchants or adventurers was unclear.

You're also named Gehrman Sparrow? A treasure map... Why does that sound like a scam... News of my killing Wormtongue has already reached this place from Bayam? Yes, it was likely carried by telegrams or travelers. Therefore, many people know my name and deeds but not what I look like... Swindlers are taking advantage of this to impersonate me and defraud others... Klein moved closer as realization dawned on him.

The green-eyed man drank a mouthful of liquor and slammed the glass onto the table.

"I don't mind whether you refuse or agree, but I hate it when people make me wait!

"Do you wish to be like Wormtongue?"

The young man opposite him said, somewhat intimidated, "I know you're a powerful adventurer..."

"So?" The green-eyed man cut off the man's words.

At that moment, his collar tightened. Someone lifted him off his feet and threw him out the door.

Klein didn't provide an explanation as he directly threw the man out of the door with a stoic expression.

Then, he drew his revolver and aimed it at the spot where the man had fallen to the ground. He pulled the trigger without any hesitation.

Bang!

As the green-eyed man hit the ground, he saw a spark flare near his crotch. Terrified, he swallowed his curses and scrambled away.

Without need for an explanation, his cowardly reaction proved that he wasn't Gehrman Sparrow.

Klein ignored the intended victims, who stood there in a daze. He politely blew across the muzzle of his revolver before slipping it back into his shoulder holster.

He then walked slowly to the bar through the ensuing silence.

Chapter 614: Adventurer Association

Klein sat in front of the bar counter and slammed his fist onto the counter.

"One glass of Southville beer."

Behind him, a few bouncers hesitated about whether to come over to give him a warning or pretend that no one had fired a shot.

The bartender signaled the bouncers with a glance as he picked up a glass and chuckled.

"Why don't you try a cup of Sweet Lemon?

"Our boss once went to Trier to learn mixology. He has mastered many of the cocktail recipes left behind by Emperor Roselle, and he personally created Oravi's most popular drink, Sweet Lemon."

Klein wasn't moved as he calmly replied, "I only drink beer."

"Alright." The bartender made no rush to fetch a bottle of Southville beer. Smiling, he asked, "Sir, are you an adventurer?"

Klein nodded without answering.

The bartender continued without hesitation, smiling as he said, "Perhaps you should meet our boss. He's very friendly to new adventurers and is willing to treat them to drinks. Besides, he can offer you some assistance."

Klein, who had come with the intention of purchasing the supplementary ingredients of a Marionettist, didn't reject the offer. He took out a brass penny and flicked it before catching it firmly.

He placed the penny on the bar as a tip to thank the bartender for the information, then slowly stood and followed a bouncer to the second floor, entering a room at the far end of the corridor.

The room was covered with thick, brownish-yellow, soft carpet. There was a faint smell of high-quality coal burning, mixed with the aroma of a cigar.

A middle-aged man with blond hair combed neatly back reclined in a chair, puffing on a cigar as he read the newspapers. Six bodyguards surrounded him.

Although Klein wasn't a Beyonder of the Spectator domain and couldn't determine the bodyguards' particular traits or strength, nor did he belong to the Mystery Pryer or Monster pathway and see secrets others couldn't discover, his spiritual intuition told him that they were definitely Beyonders--Beyonders who posed some degree of threat to him.

The middle-aged man put down the newspaper and tugged at the collar of his black shirt before slowly standing. He extended his right hand and said, "Welcome to Oravi, my adventurer friend."

His blue eyes had a smile to them as he looked rather sincere.

Klein extended his right hand and shook his without a word, waiting for the man to speak.

The middle-aged man pointed to a sofa opposite the reclining chair and chuckled.

"I'm the boss here, Bilt Brando.

"I was once an adventurer of considerable strength. I've survived to this day and earned quite a bit."

He's very confident in his strength, daring to state it so openly... Klein didn't stand on ceremony as he sat down. He leaned slightly forward and waited expressionlessly for Bilt to speak.

Receiving no response from the adventurer, Bilt sat again and drew on his cigar before saying leisurely, "To be honest, I have an ulterior motive for meeting every new adventurer."

He pointed to the sea chart on the desk and said in a resonant voice, "East of the Rorsted Archipelago, the seas grow increasingly chaotic. The military and the Church have less and less power, until they can protect little beyond their own harbors.

"The sea around here is a pirate playground, extremely disadvantageous to us adventurers. Therefore, I wish to organize everyone to form a loosely connected alliance, so that we can band together when faced with danger.

"This alliance has no strictly binding terms. You can choose to turn a blind eye when other adventurers encounter danger, and no one will punish you. However, if you encounter a similar situation, don't expect anyone to help you.

"In addition, I can promise this: members of the alliance will receive my lowest prices when buying from me, and at least the market price when selling their spoils."

After finishing the description, Bilt smiled at the new adventurer. He chuckled and asked, "What do you think of it? Are you interested in joining this loose alliance? Don't worry. You won't need to make any additional payment. Our purpose is only to render assistance when faced with pirates."

Klein nodded.

"It sounds pretty good."

"Haha, I think so too. I once considered naming this association the Adventuring Brotherhood, but I felt that 'brotherhood' was too restrictive, so I changed it to the Adventurer Association," Bilt said self-deprecatingly.

Having said that, he lowered his hand with the cigar and smacked his lips. He shook his head and smiled.

"Sorry, I forgot to ask your name."

Klein maintained his posture and replied in a deep voice, "Gehrman Sparrow."

"Gehrman Sparrow..." Bilt's eyelids jumped as his smile instantly stiffened. The guards around him clearly tensed up as though they were facing a formidable enemy.

Bilt quickly composed himself. Without trying to verify whether this was the real Gehrman Sparrow or merely an impostor, he chuckled and said, "Is there anything you wish to buy? I have quite a lot of good stuff here."

"Sonia's Golden Spring water," Klein said with a calm expression while inwardly feeling cautious.

He didn't mention the drago tree bark since there was a high chance of buying the ingredient at herb stores.

Bilt Brando heaved a sigh of relief and smiled.

"Easy.

"30 pounds for 100 ml.

"Trust me. Even if you head to Sonia Island, it will only be a few pounds cheaper than this price. Those barbarians from Feysac have sealed off the fountain's source, so it's difficult for others to get any of it."

"Deal." Klein had a rough idea of the price of the Golden Spring water, and he knew that 30 pounds for 100 ml was indeed rather cheap.

After completing the deal, Bilt was just about to speak after some deliberation when he suddenly heard knocking.

After receiving his permission, a bouncer entered and leaned over, whispering into his ear.

Bilt's expression instantly sank as his entire person appeared abnormally cold. He suffused an indescribable killing intent.

Indeed, he possesses impressive strength. If he wasn't a famous adventurer in the past, he must've been a famous pirate... He should be a Mid-Sequence Beyonder... Klein calmly assessed him.

Bilt turned his head and said to Klein with some difficulty, "Mr. Sparrow, I have matters to tend to.

"We can have drinks together if the opportunity arises."

"Okay." Klein slowly got up, without any intention of involving himself in Bilt's matters.

Of course, this didn't mean that he wasn't curious, but curiosity was never something that could change the way he did things.

After leaving Sweet Lemon, Klein successfully returned to his inn.

City of Silver, during the "daytime" marked by frequent lightning.

After Derrick Berg finished practicing the various Beyonder powers of a Solar High Priest, he followed the edge of the training field toward the twin towers.

Chapter 615: Grayish-white Fog

While Miss Justice exchanged greetings with everyone, The Hermit Cattleya remained silent and observed as before. She didn't involve herself, as though she were an outsider at the gathering.

With three pages of Roselle's diary in hand, Klein wasn't as eager to read them as before. "However, looking down from the peak, the abyss appeared endless and boundless. I joked with Edwards that if I committed suicide by jumping off that cliff, I might never reach the ground and would remain in a state of perpetual free fall."

Upon reading this, Klein nearly raised his eyebrows. He couldn't believe that Roselle would dare organize his knights and sailors to explore the region's periphery after seeing the Abyss.

Isn't he afraid of dying? Legend has it that it's a place capable of corrupting everything and causing all life to perish! At this stage, Roselle definitely hadn't reached Sequence 4 and wasn't a demigod. He was at best Sequence 5, or even lower... If it were me, I definitely would've turned the ship around and reported it to the Church... At that moment, Klein gained a profound understanding of the vast difference between Roselle and himself.

In addition, Roselle's description of the Abyss's periphery reminded Klein of the mysterious stone doors in Amon's mausoleum. A similar scene had appeared after the puppet of Horamick, an archbishop of the Church of Steam and Machinery, touched them.

I wonder what Roselle discovered there... At the very least, he didn't die from his exploration. He still had an exciting life after that... Klein moved his gaze down and read the remaining content on the diary page.

"24th April. We proceeded down the peak in an attempt to probe deeper.

"It's very quiet, and we didn't discover anything."

"25th April. We saw devils, but they were in the form of rotting corpses.

"26th April. There is nothing but corpses and silence, with no end in sight.

What happened after this? Did Emperor Roselle successfully escape the Abyss's periphery, or did he encounter something else? What did the abnormality there signify? An intense battle? Klein felt a trace of anticipation as he turned to the second page, only to be disappointed that it wasn't connected.

"8th May. My beloved Bernadette is almost two. The older she grows, the more lovable she becomes. She's truly a young lady who lives up to the excellent genes of her mother and me.

"Hearing her clearly call me daddy and seeing her bubbly figure, I suddenly felt a sense of satisfaction.

On the one hand, this is because I find it very difficult to curb my desires, making me susceptible to the influence of my environment. I might even be killed instantly.

I can devote some emotion to them, but never anything deep. Therefore, I can join all kinds of depraved gatherings without guilt. I can treat people I know well with cold ruthlessness. Without children, it's impossible to understand this feeling. No, I don't even have a girlfriend... However, I have to remain vigilant against the state of mind mentioned in this entry... It might not have existed in the past, but that doesn't mean it won't appear in the future. The higher the Sequence, the more one's godhood outweighs one's humanity... Grayish-white fog? The Emperor saw an endless grayish-white fog after replicating the mysterious silver plate from Earth! Klein's eyes suddenly shrank when he read the diary entry.

There happened to be an unchanging, endless gray fog beneath his feet!

Could our transmigrations be connected to this mysterious space? But why couldn't the Emperor enter when I can? According to the diary entries from the final stages of his life, he definitely never unraveled the mystery of the silver plate. Otherwise, he would've thought of this place in his moment of utter despair and madness. He would've used it as a final recourse... Klein raised his head and looked beyond the ancient palace.

Does the secret of our transmigration lie there? Klein made the diary pages in his hand vanish, leaned back in his chair, and said evenly, "You may begin."

So this is a silver lining? Fors was stunned.

Mr. Hanged Man makes a great deal of sense... When it comes to analyzing events and circumstances, I'm still far from his equal... Audrey felt somewhat dejected. She even considered puffing out her cheeks, but immediately realized that it wasn't an elegant gesture. All she could do was keep the thought to herself.

For the rest of the members, Emlyn, who didn't know much about Lovia, had a hard time keeping up. However, he knew about Jack and the repeated cycles, so he was barely able to understand the discussion.

To escape the City of Silver's predicament, they released a "danger" involving the True Creator? Emlyn speculated inwardly.

Unlike him, Cattleya couldn't understand a word. She felt as though she inhabited a completely different world from the other members.

I actually feel admiration despite not understanding what they're saying... The last time I felt this way was several years ago, when I was still very weak... There were hints of it in previous Tarot Gatherings, especially when matters involved the City of Silver, but never as strongly as this time...

Also, why are they discussing the True Creator and Blasphemer Amon so naturally? Eh... It should be the Blasphemer Amon I know of... They're only Mid-Sequence Beyonders... What have they actually done in the past? Cattleya's confidence as a powerhouse suffered a setback at that very moment.

After hearing Mr. Hanged Man's analysis, Derrick first sighed in relief, then asked nervously, "Could this result in our City of Silver being destroyed by the Fallen Creator?"

He had hoped Mr. Hanged Man would offer a reassuring answer, only to hear him say gravely, "That possibility can't be ruled out."

"Then what should I do?" Derrick earnestly sought advice.

"Without knowing what your Chief and the other elders of the six-member council are planning, I cannot offer any suggestions, but I don't think you need to panic so much." Alger raised his head and glanced at Mr. Fool, who silently observed the members from the end of the long bronze table.

Following his gaze, Derrick instinctively looked over and saw Mr. Fool's indistinct figure shrouded in gray fog.

He instantly felt a sense of serenity, and he couldn't help but recall that holy and powerful angel.

...Although I've foiled several of the True Creator's plots, I wouldn't last a second in a real clash with "Him"... It would end with the True Creator arriving at The Fool's doorstep, only to discover that The Fool was completely hollow... Klein continued listening casually, neither speaking nor betraying any emotion.

Derrick retracted his gaze and said to Alger, "Mr. Hanged Man, thank you for your help. I will try to figure out what Chief and the others are planning."

As the matter drew to a close, Cattleya deliberated before asking, "By the Blasphemer Amon you mentioned earlier, were you referring to the ancestor of the Amon family from the Fourth Epoch's Tudor Empire, the son of the Creator?"

Son of the Creator? Audrey was stunned by what Ma'am Hermit said. She instinctively turned her head to the end of the long bronze table and discovered Mr. Fool sitting there calmly, as though he was tacitly confirming Amon's other identity.

Chapter 617: The Mysterious Adam

The son of the Creator? Alger was stunned by The Hermit's description, and his brows twitched involuntarily.

However, he didn't directly inquire further because this could very likely expose certain matters. Besides, he was very confident that there would be other members asking.

"The son of the Creator?" Fors asked with piqued interest.

She never expected that Amon had such a shocking identity, aside from the titles like Blasphemer and King of Angels. She was filled with curiosity over this.

Cattleya glanced at her and said without changing her tone, "Legend has it that when the original Creator split into the various deities and races, 'His' body produced two babies. One of them was Blasphemer Amon."

The mural inside Amon's mausoleum was somewhat different from your description. When Amon and Adam were born, the Creator had just awakened and reclaimed the ancient gods' authorities. "He" sat high upon a holy mountain, surrounded by angels who sang his praises. There was no sign of him dying or splitting apart...

That mural does have something in common with the City of Silver's historical records... Perhaps the Creator truly awakened twice. And perhaps the original Creator and Amon's father--the Creator recorded by the City of Silver--aren't the same person, but have a relationship of succession... As Klein listened to Ma'am Hermit's description, he began theorizing.

Amon is actually the son of the Creator... That status is in no way inferior to the true gods! Yet Mr. Fool and our Tarot Club easily purified "His" avatar and ended "His" possession... Recalling those events, Audrey suddenly felt that she had unknowingly surpassed many ordinary Beyonders. The circles they moved in and the levels of knowledge to which they were exposed were worlds apart!

She asked with piqued interest, "Ma'am Hermit, do you know the other son of the Creator?"

"Adam," Cattleya answered succinctly.

Audrey was clearly taken aback. Her question had only required a "yes" or "no." After confirming that Cattleya knew, she had planned to ask for details and make it clear that she was willing to pay. Who would've expected Ma'am Hermit to give her the name Adam directly?

She doesn't seem to value such information too highly... She has access to a wealth of information... Audrey inferred several things from this detail as her eyes darted about.

"I've never heard of an important figure named Adam."

"I haven't either. Apart from being recorded as the son of the Creator, it's like 'He' has never appeared before. There are nearly no traces of 'Him' in the history of the Fourth Epoch," Cattleya answered frankly.

This Adam is very mysterious... I'm already rather well versed in the Fourth Epoch's three empires--Solomon, Tudor, and Trunsoest--as well as the angel families beneath them. Yet I've never heard of "Him." If Arrodes hadn't shown me the Church of Steam and Machinery's exploration of Amon's mausoleum, I wouldn't even know "His" name... Did "He" perish long ago, or is "He" hiding in the shadows and plotting something? Klein sighed inwardly.

Audrey subconsciously glanced at Mr. Hanged Man and discovered him shaking his head as well.

Amon is a King of Angels, so it wouldn't make sense for Adam not to be one. The City of Silver should have records of him... Audrey looked at Derrick.

For some reason, Derrick easily understood what was on Miss Justice's mind. He said in slight embarrassment, "We do not know about Adam, just like how we don't know about Amon.

"I previously searched for ancient tomes related to the King of Angels. I believe I lacked sufficient clearance because my Sequence was still low at the time. I will continue searching for information about this."

King of Angels... Cattleya moved her fingers indiscernibly as she memorized the phrase.

She knew that Miss Justice would definitely answer if she asked, so without waiting, she directly asked, "Amon is a King of Angels that all of you are referring to?"

"Yes." To thank Ma'am Hermit for her generosity, Audrey explained in detail, "In the records of the City of Silver, the Creator had many angels attending to 'Him.' Among them, the leaders of the angels who were closest to the throne of God were deemed King of Angels.

"We haven't managed to confirm many Kings of Angels. One is the Angel of Fate, Ouroboros, who appears in the City of Silver's ancient tomes. 'His' title is Tail Devourer. The second is Medici, whom Mr. Fool once mentioned. We still don't know 'His' title. The third is only a conjecture. We suspect that Amon is the Angel of Time among the Kings of Angels. Since he is a King of Angels, Adam, who is also a son of the Creator, is very likely one as well."

Haven't managed to confirm many... I didn't even know the term 'King of Angels,' yet you've already identified half of 'Them.' That's not many? None of you are at a higher Sequence than me... Cattleya was nearly speechless.

Only then did Emlyn truly understand what a King of Angels was. He decided that, after returning to the real world, he would use these names to search the Sanguine's secret historical records for traces of the Kings of Angels.

Cattleya fell silent for two seconds before saying, "Mr. Sun, you were once possessed by Amon?"

"Yes; it was 'His' avatar," Derrick answered honestly. "However, 'He' was purified by Mr. Fool. I wasn't injured at all."

Purified by Mr. Fool... Cattleya couldn't hold back the urge to look at the end of the long bronze table.

She had imagined that she possessed a general understanding of Mr. Fool's state, believing that "He" was still reawakening and unable to use much of "His" power. That was why "He" had assumed the identity of the Sea God and obtained the scepter. However, the present situation shook the foundations of her speculation. Mr. Fool was like an iceberg: what lay hidden beneath the surface was even more terrifying and unfathomable!

"He" easily thwarted Amon's plans... Under certain situations, "He" can briefly break through the restrictions and produce strength at the level of a god? Cattleya's heart sank as she didn't ask further. She cautiously shut her mouth.

At this moment, Audrey had thought of something that puzzled her because of this topic. She proactively asked, "Ma'am Hermit, after Amon's avatar was purified by Mr. Fool, a worm with twelve transparent rings was left behind. It's said to be a Worm of Time. Is that correct?"

Cattleya thought for two seconds before saying, "...Yes."

"Which pathway does it belong to? I mean, which Beyonder pathway does the Blasphemer belong to?" Audrey pressed curiously.

Cattleya answered directly without any thought, "The Marauder pathway.

"Legend has it that at the Saint or Angel level, they are able to fool fate and cheat the rules, becoming a parasite of time."

Sounds very impressive... Audrey marveled inwardly before asking, "Then, what can a Worm of Time be used for?"

Nice question! Klein secretly cheered for Miss Justice.

He pumped himself up as he waited for Ma'am Hermit to give the answer.

He happened to have a Worm of Time in his junk pile!

"It's a material rich in spirituality and unique effects. It can be used in particular rituals or to create high-level charms. But as for how, I'm not sure." After answering, Cattleya suddenly sighed.

She recalled the nightmare of being constantly pursued by knowledge and the vast flood of information that had seemed capable of bursting her skull.

But even so, she still didn't know a lot.

If the Hidden Sage hadn't gone mad and could provide knowledge bit by bit, I'd actually be more than happy to learn from "Him"... Cattleya secretly sighed.

All that matters is that it's useful... As for how it's used, I have other people to consult... Klein controlled The World with great satisfaction and looked at Emlyn.

"Mr. Moon, I have something I would like to communicate with you in private."

Aren't the transactions over? Emlyn nodded, feeling somewhat uncertain.

"Okay."

The World immediately requested Mr. Fool's permission and successfully obtained the privilege of isolating the others.

Then he looked at Emlyn White and said, "I want you to show me every action Leonard Mitchell took in your memories."

"Why?" Emlyn asked, puzzled.

"It involves certain secrets," Klein controlled The World to answer succinctly.

His true goal wasn't to buy that mystical item, but to observe Leonard Mitchell? That fellow harbors deep secrets... The World does as well. He was willing to spend 7,500 pounds on an investigation! However, that mystical item is indeed powerful. Isn't The World constantly seeking a way to eliminate the mental corruption in a Beyonder characteristic? This will likely prove useful... Emlyn suppressed his wandering thoughts and carefully recalled Leonard Mitchell from his own perspective. With Mr. Fool's help, he conjured the memory as a stream of light.

When the streaming light landed in The World's hands, Klein quickly browsed through it.

He confirmed that Leonard truly knew of Grandpa's existence, but couldn't determine whether he understood the danger.

Temporarily suppressing any corresponding thoughts, Klein removed the isolation barriers and allowed the Tarot Club members to continue.

At this moment, Audrey was reminded of something by Mr. World's private communication. She hurriedly looked at Fors and asked in concern, "Miss Magician, did you discover any abnormalities at Williams Street?"

"That will require Mr. World to answer you," Fors said, having been put into a difficult position.

Without waiting for Miss Justice to speak again, Klein controlled The World and hoarsely replied, "There were abnormalities.

"But I have already informed the Church of Evernight and the Church of Steam.

"If there are any future problems, I might require your help."

"Alright!" Audrey heaved a sigh of relief.

Informed the Churches of Evernight and Steam... This... Cattleya found the description ludicrous.

From what she knew, a secret organization like the Tarot Club was a natural enemy of the official Beyonders. There was no room for any compromises between the two, but the way The World had dealt with it and the words he used had made it seem like the Tarot Club was an organization that cooperated with the official Beyonders...

Colored by these feelings, she listened to the rest of the conversation before bowing and bidding farewell when Mr. Fool made the closing remark.

Soon, Klein was the only one left above the gray fog.

Chapter 618: Volunteer Work

On the second floor of the Sweet Lemon Bar, in the owner's office.

Bilt Brando stood by the window holding a cigar. He stared outside with unfocused eyes and a dark, terrifying expression.

At that moment, a bodyguard entered, bowed slightly, and said carefully, "Sir, Sothoth has returned from the east."

"Let him in." Bilt tried hard to correct his expression.

Sothoth Yann was his assistant, an important member of the Adventurer Association.

In less than a minute, Sothoth walked in, dressed in a linen shirt, brown jacket, and red headscarf. He looked to be in his thirties and had bronzed skin, deep-set eyes, and black facial hair around his lips. He was clearly someone who spent most of his time at sea.

Sothoth bowed rather informally and sized up Bilt Brando. "Boss, something happened?"

"Yes, something happened. From the looks of it, the plan will fail." Bilt didn't conceal the matter from him. He sighed. "I have no idea how I'm going to answer that important figure."

Without waiting for Sothoth to answer, he asked, "Have there been any changes at the eastern front?"

"Much the same as usual. The pirates still pursue every ship they can plunder. They even target one another. The navy can only guard the various colonial outposts, barely keeping the sea routes open while protecting the more important ships. Sea battles occur often, with each side claiming victories," Sothoth said with a shrug.

"The eastern front of the Sonia Sea is a pirate's playground..." Bilt sighed in agreement.

Sothoth thought for a moment and added, "There has been some recent news from the islands on the eastern front. Apparently, it first came from the Black Death."

"Vice Admiral Ailment? What news is it?" Bilt asked, his interest piqued.

Sothoth said with a mixture of solemnity and excitement, "Vice Admiral Ailment really was the target of an assassination attempt and suffered serious injuries. And the person who attacked her was the adventurer Gehrman Sparrow!"

"Gehrman Sparrow?" Bilt blurted out.

"Yes, it's him! He truly is a powerhouse at the level of a pirate admiral! Even if it was a sneak attack, it happened aboard the Black Death. Despite all the infamous pirates around, he escaped successfully after dealing a heavy blow to Vice Admiral Ailment. He later hunted down Wormtongue Mithor," Sothoth confirmed with a sigh.

Bilt staggered before sighing.

"That's important news.

"There are very few pirate admiral-level powerhouses among adventurers. Dealing a heavy blow to a pirate admiral alone aboard her flagship--only someone supremely confident or sufficiently insane could attempt such a feat. Only a madman would infiltrate a pirate admiral's flagship to assassinate her instead of choosing another location!"

Having said that, his expression changed slightly.

"I met an adventurer named Gehrman Sparrow last night."

"For real?" Sothoth's pupils shrank as he asked solemnly.

"I can't be sure since I've never met the real Gehrman Sparrow, or seen his photograph or portrait." Bilt shook his head.

Sothoth thought for a moment and said, "You can search for newspapers from the Rorsted Archipelago to confirm his identity. So many days have passed that travelers should've brought over the relevant issues of the News Report and Sonia Morning Post. Yes, government offices, police stations, churches, and charitable organizations subscribe to the Rorsted Archipelago's major newspapers."

The Rorsted Archipelago was the Loen Kingdom's largest colony in the Central Sonia Sea. Its influence radiated outward, so Oravi Island, only three days away by sea, undoubtedly fell within its sphere. Official organizations and churches all subscribed to the area's newspapers and magazines, so any nonurgent news arrived within three or four days.

"Alright." Bilt nodded and pressed for details. "Do you know exactly how Gehrman Sparrow attempted to assassinate Vice Admiral Ailment?"

Sothoth thought for a moment and said, "It's said that Gehrman Sparrow is able to change into anyone, just like Vice Admiral Hurricane Qilangos.

"It's with that power that he successfully infiltrated the Black Death and found an opportunity to carry out the assassination."

"He can change into anyone..." Bilt's eyes lit up.

No, that won't do. That's a crazy guy who dares to infiltrate the Black Death to assassinate Vice Admiral Ailment. He causes one to instinctively fear him and distance themselves from him... The light in Bilt's eyes dimmed.

Furthermore, I don't even know if he's the real one or not... He subconsciously shook his head.

I wonder when the Nighthawks and Machinery Hivemind will take action and deal with the abnormality at Williams Street. I hope they do it as soon as possible... Amidst his thoughts, Klein left the gray fog and returned to the real world.

After some consideration, he took out a piece of paper and laid it out on a brown desk.

He wrote with a dark red fountain pen, asking about Mr. Azik's recent situation before mentioning how he had discovered that someone had a parasite in him while he was searching for a mystical item that could steal the Beyonder powers of others.

He then seemingly asked in passing whether there was a way to bypass the parasite and warn the host.

Using this as an opening, he added that he had learned some information about the Worm of Time, which was related to High-Sequence Beyonders of the Marauder pathway. He also mentioned that he knew such an item could serve as a sacrificial offering in important rituals or as material for high-level charms. However, he had no idea how to make them.

Phew... Klein put down the fountain pen, folded the letter, and brought the copper whistle to his lips. He blew at it forcefully.

White bones spewed out like a fountain, forming a gigantic skeletal messenger. This time, however, the messenger didn't burrow up from downstairs. Instead, as it had many times before, it tore through the ceiling and looked down at its summoner from above.

Klein knew that this wasn't because the messenger had become impolite again, but because he was staying on the first floor of an inn...

He flicked his wrist and threw the letter like a dart, making it land accurately in the messenger's huge bony hand.

The flames in the messenger's eye sockets flickered as though it was observing Klein, but ultimately, nothing happened.

Its body disintegrated into a waterfall made of bones as they drilled into the ground.

After everything was done, Klein didn't unfold the paper crane. Instead, he erased what he had written and copied the same message to seek advice from the Snake of Mercury, Will Auceptin.

This was because he had realized something terrible. The paper crane wasn't a mystical item or Beyonder weapon, but an ordinary folded piece of paper. After its writing had been erased repeatedly, the paper was beginning to lose its structural integrity. After a few more attempts, it might tear outright.

I'll save it for matters important enough to warrant contact--for example, if Mr. Azik doesn't know how to bypass Grandpa and warn Leonard... Klein shook his head silently and quickly put away the items on his desk.

In addition, he hadn't dared use the radio transceiver to contact Arrodes recently. The powerhouse sent by the True Creator was likely still roaming the area in search of the All-Black Eye's aura. The "scent" of the gray fog was equally capable of attracting the True Creator's attention, allowing "Him" to alert his believers.

Today, I'll continue sightseeing and relax. Tomorrow, I'll start looking for a chance to truly act! Klein gathered his thoughts, put on his coat, took his top hat, and left the inn.

He planned on heading to the mountains outside Oravi Harbor to watch the sunset!

The idea came from a popular novel by Leeann Mastaing. Born in Odora, the gentleman had settled permanently in Backlund after turning twenty. His books described the sunset over Mt. Saint Draco with profound feeling, calling it the most beautiful sight he had ever seen.

Klein took a carriage out of the city and walked to the foot of Mt. Saint Draco. It took him an hour to reach the summit of the modest mountain.

Time passed as the sun slowly set, making the blue sea to the left of the mountain peak resemble a sea of fire. To the right, the emerald-green forests and vast fields appeared gilded.

All the colors bloomed with a final touch of radiance before darkness gradually descended.

Ships entered the harbor as carriages drove into the city. The busy people began returning home on the roads parallel to the wheat fields and fruit gardens.

When darkness enveloped the entire land, specks of warm light lit up one after another inside and outside the city. They were like resplendent gems that dotted the velvet night sky.

It's truly beautiful... Klein admired the view for a moment, the lights of countless homes reflected in his eyes.

He turned in silence and descended the mountain path, accompanied by the dark trees. After reaching the foot of the mountain, he walked some distance before hiring a carriage on the outskirts of the port city.

The carriage rolled steadily onward. The dim yellow halos cast by elegant, iron-black streetlamps illuminated the ground before receding into the distance behind him.

After some time, Klein returned to his inn. He took out his key and opened his door.

A bed, a desk, and a chair stood motionless in the deep darkness of the room, faintly reflecting the crimson glow.

Klein closed the door very gently and walked to the window. He stood in the shadows created by the curtains and remained motionless for quite some time.

The lights outside were still bright.

Early the next morning.

Klein turned on the tap and patted his face with ice-cold water, rejuvenating his entire body.

He had already thought of a way to engage in true acting.

It was still the hospital where death could occur at any moment!

In the past, Klein had only wandered the area without a clear focus, making it difficult to find suitable targets. This time, he planned to volunteer so he could remain in the hospital for extended periods. He could provide hospice care to dying patients whose families were temporarily absent. In doing so, he could wait for the targets he needed.

After having breakfast, Klein arrived at 10 Blackforest Street and entered the Oravi Hospice Foundation.

This was a charity organization of the Church of the Evernight Goddess. One of its responsibilities was to provide the various hospitals with trained volunteers.

Klein came to the registration booth and saw the female employee reading the papers. Hence, he lightly tapped the table to attract her attention.

"Is there something I can help you with?" the lady asked, lowering the newspapers.

"I wish to do some volunteer work," Klein said succinctly.

"Name?" The lady looked up at him.

Suddenly, her eyes froze as her right hand trembled. The fountain pen she had just picked up fell to the ground.

In the papers in front of her was a portrait that looked almost realistic.

The portrait's owner was that of the crazy and dangerous adventurer, Gehrman Sparrow!

Chapter 619: Unable to Speak

Oravi Hospice Foundation.

The lady responsible for volunteer registration hurriedly bent down and frantically searched for the fallen fountain pen. Klein then noticed that she was reading an issue of the News Report from several days ago, when his portrait and exploits had been published.

They subscribe to newspapers from the Rorsted Archipelago here as well? Given the sailing time between the two places, they can indeed only read news from three or four days ago... If I'd known, I would've adopted a new face and invented a false name before coming... Klein stood before the desk, thinking helplessly.

The lady in her thirties finally picked up the fountain pen, raised her head, and said with a trembling voice, "Y-you want to do volunteer work?"

"Yes," Klein answered.

"B-but you're an adventurer," the terrified lady stammered, searching for an excuse.

She instinctively didn't want such an extremely dangerous person to do volunteer work.

Being famous isn't necessarily a good thing... Forget it. I'll switch my appearance and name before coming again... Klein had the intention of giving up as he questioned her without any emotion, "Who made the rules that adventurers can't do volunteer work?"

The lady responsible for the registration was on the brink of tears as she blurted out, "It wasn't me!"

The volunteer registration room fell unnaturally silent. Klein was taken aback before feeling an urge to laugh. It took considerable effort to maintain Gehrman Sparrow's image.

After the lady calmed down, she sensed that her answer was clearly problematic. She forced a smile and said, "No, I mean this isn't regulated by anyone.

"My impression of adventurers is that they are very busy. They need to be out at sea and have very little time to do volunteer work.

"That's them," Klein answered succinctly.

The lady covered her mouth with one hand and smiled.

"Alright. I'll immediately help you with your registration."

As she spoke, she pulled out a form and handed it over.

"Please fill it in. We will provide the corresponding training and volunteer jobs according to your requests.

"We will contact you, or you can come by at any time to ask for updates."

She had already decided not to place Gehrman Sparrow's form in the docket. Instead, she would hand it directly to the person in charge of the foundation and the police.

Goddess, why is this dangerous fellow here to do volunteer work? She secretly drew a crimson moon on her chest.

Klein nodded in silence. He took the form and sat down. Picking up a fountain pen, he began filling in his basic information.

As he did so, a man in a doctor's coat entered and asked, "Joanna, are there any new volunteers? We're about to begin the morning training."

Joanna, who was in charge of registration, intended to shake her head. However, even though Gehrman Sparrow sat silently opposite her without looking up, he placed her under immense pressure. She didn't dare lie.

"There is," she first replied to the man and then looked at Klein. "Mr. Sparrow, do you wish to receive the basic training for volunteer work now, or do you wish to wait until tomorrow?"

Klein thought for a moment, then said, "Now."

He planned to familiarize himself with the work first so that, after changing his identity, he could demonstrate his competence as a volunteer. That way, he could quickly begin helping at a hospital.

Joanna clearly drew a breath and said, "Then follow Mr. Gravia after you are done filling in the form."

"Alright," Klein replied calmly.

Half an hour later, the calm and polite adventurer, whose madness lay hidden within him, stared at the exceptionally filthy toilet before him. Holding his breath, he asked, "Scrub it?"

"Right. You've already received basic training on how to carry a patient. However, that isn't our most common task at a hospital. We're mainly responsible for cleaning up patients' vomit, changing and washing linens, and keeping the washrooms clean. Heh heh, matters such as bandaging wounds are left to the professionals. You only need to master the basics." Gravia pinched his nose and pointed at the stained toilets. "A volunteer has already demonstrated it for you. Please begin."

This is very different from what I imagined... Completely different... Klein's first reaction was to turn his back and leave, but he ultimately picked up the cleaning tools with a deadpan expression. He held back his disgust and went over before crouching down.

Then, he leaned back a little as he extended his right arm.

At noon, Klein took off his white gown and wore his top hat before leaving Oravi Hospice Foundation with a cold expression.

He had serious second thoughts about continuing the volunteer work to find opportunities for real acting.

Only when his carriage reached the Sweet Lemon Bar did he steel his resolve to continue.

I have to become a Marionettist in 1350 and digest the potion so that I can begin seeking clues to becoming a High-Sequence Beyonder, Klein reminded himself once more.

He reined in his emotions and entered the Sweet Lemon Bar. He spent 8 pence on apple-cider-marinated pork ribs and a piece of buttered bread.

Together with a cup of rye beer that cost 1.5 pence, these formed Klein's lunch.

He ate at an unhurried pace, then wiped his mouth with a handkerchief and asked the bartender, "Where's your boss? I have something to discuss with him."

Since he had already verbally agreed to join the Adventurer Association, Klein naturally intended to make use of the loose alliance. He planned to ask Bilt Brando to contact an unaffiliated Artisan or one willing to take private commissions. He wanted an Artisan to create an item like Azik's copper whistle so that he wouldn't need to set up a ritual and chant the incantation every time he summoned his messenger. The process was complicated, troublesome, and time-consuming.

Of course, if Bilt Brando didn't know an Artisan, Klein didn't plan on forcing the matter. After all, joining the Adventurer Association didn't require him to pay.

The bartender pointed to the staircase and said, "He's on the second floor.

"If you've already agreed to join his association and have seen those bodyguards, then they'll let you up."

Klein gave a subtle nod, slowly rose, and walked toward the staircase.

Indeed, no guards stopped him. Only when he reached the second floor did a bodyguard approach him to inquire about his purpose.

Thanks to his reputation as Gehrman Sparrow, he easily met Bilt Brando again. He also saw an unfamiliar man beside him.

"My friend, Sothoth Yann, an important member of the association." Bilt pointed to the man in a red headscarf.

At the same time, he exchanged looks with the man and saw the seriousness and puzzlement in each other's eyes.

After a brief greeting, Klein took a chair and said directly, "I wish to obtain an Artisan's help. I wonder if the association can provide any leads."

"An Artisan?" Bilt drew on his cigar and paused for several seconds. After a moment's consideration, he said, "I do know an Artisan, but he's unwilling to meet strangers. That would place him in grave danger. If you have a request, I can act as the intermediary. I believe my reputation is sufficient."

To most Churches, Mid- and Low-Sequence Beyonders without malicious intentions could sometimes be ignored. Artisans, however, had to be dealt with whenever discovered. Otherwise, the mystical items they created could flood the market and cause extreme unrest in human society.

From the looks of it, the Adventurer Association Bilt organized isn't merely an empty venture. At the very least, it has amassed considerable resources and numerous information channels... Klein took out the murloc bladder he had prepared long ago.

"I wish to make this into a ring, mainly to provide one with underwater mobility."

He temporarily didn't mention the messenger, planning to test the Artisan's ability and Bilt's reputation. If the messenger-summoning incantation became widely known, it would create quite a bit of trouble.

A Sequence 9 Beyonder characteristic isn't expensive. Even if Bilt succumbs to greed and seizes the finished mystical item, it doesn't matter. After all, I have plenty of charms from the Sea God domain... Furthermore, it's not as though I couldn't find him to settle the debt. That might be even better. If he can organize an Adventurer Association, he must have plenty of money and ingredients... As Klein's thoughts raced, he couldn't help sizing up Bilt.

In that instant, Bilt shuddered for some baffling reason. All the hair on his back stood up.

Gehrman Sparrow looked at him as though he had discovered a treasure trove!

As treasure, this wasn't a good experience at all.

Bilt glanced at Sothoth and forced a smile.

"This is a murloc's bladder, right?"

"Making a material at this level into a mystical item basically wouldn't have any accidents.

"The Artisan's fee is 150 pounds. You can pay me after it's crafted."

A very fair price... Klein nodded silently and tossed over the murloc bladder.

After Bilt caught it, he added, "Ask the Artisan whether he can inscribe the summoning ritual for a spirit world creature onto an item that will remain usable for at least a year."

"No problem." Bilt sighed in relief before signaling Sothoth with a glance.

Sothoth rubbed his sunken eye socket and stepped forward.

"Mr. Gehrman, would you be interested in heading out to sea soon?

"We already have several decent ships. They're planning to hire crews and head to the eastern front to hunt pirates."

Interesting... but my focus right now is to digest my potion... Klein calmly shook his head.

Sothoth's smile froze before vanishing.

He said nothing further, as this was a rejection from an adventurer at the level of a pirate admiral. He and Bilt had already found the relevant newspapers and confirmed Gehrman Sparrow's identity.

Klein slowly stood, removed his top hat, and pressed it to his chest before bowing slightly.

"Thank you for your help."

Bilt's facial muscles twitched as though he were holding something back. He wanted to say something but couldn't bring himself to voice it.

Finally, he slowly drew in a breath and smiled.

"I believe we should hope for a pleasant partnership."

Klein sensed Bilt's and Sothoth's abnormality, but he suppressed his puzzlement and didn't ask.

I can't ask. There will be trouble once I ask... I have to focus on digesting the potion... Klein turned around and walked to the door before turning the doorknob.

"Mr. Gehrman," Bilt suddenly said.

The corner of Klein's mouth twitched as he calmly looked back.

"Nothing. Haha, I mean, as a member of the association, you can get cheaper drinks here," Bilt said with a forced smile.

Do you think I would've pressed on the matter? Tsk... Klein gently nodded and opened the door to leave.

After watching his back vanish, Bilt stared in a daze for two seconds before letting out a long sigh.

Chapter 620: Stand-in Mission

Inside the room, Sothoth pressed down on the edges of his eyes when he heard Bilt's sigh. He said in a deep voice, "Boss, that important figure will arrive the day after tomorrow."

Bilt's expression turned serious as he looked back. "I know."

"We only have two choices. First, we entrust Gehrman Sparrow with the mission regardless of whether he's trustworthy and hope for the best. Second, we immediately abandon our business in Oravi and leave with our cash and jewelry to become pirates. I'm confident I can persuade most of the sailors and adventurers aboard the Pirate Hunter to follow us." Sothoth paused before adding, "Boss, when you were an adventurer leading our ship and hunting pirates, you were always this decisive and tenacious. I believe you've already made your decision. There's nothing more I need to do."

"Haha, there's no need to flatter me." Bilt smiled bitterly. "I'm no longer the fearless pirate hunter who braved blood and gunfire. Years of easy, comfortable living have dulled my will. I don't even want to seek opportunities to advance. The companions who turned into monsters still haunt my thoughts."

Phew... He exhaled, his expression abruptly turning solemn.

"However, you're right. We don't have time to hesitate. We have to immediately make a decision."

With that, Bilt turned and gestured toward the window with his chin.

Sothoth paused, then understood what his boss meant.

After leaving the Sweet Lemon Bar, Klein strolled along the street, planning to hire a carriage at the crossroads.

At that moment, he heard a window open overhead and saw a figure leap down.

Perhaps because of the terrifying incidents involving the Antigonus family's notebook and Bansy Harbor's strange telegraph office, Klein's first thought was that Bilt Brando had suddenly died after his visit and been thrown from upstairs, plummeting toward the ground with his eyes wide open.

This was just like how he saw Ray Bieber's mother dead in a reclining chair back then. The body was highly decomposed, and the eyeball fell to the floor because of the external disturbance.

These thoughts flashed through Klein's mind before he recognized the falling figure as Sothoth Yann, not Bilt Brando.

Moreover, he looked normal and was clearly alive.

Oh no, here comes trouble... Klein's heart skipped a beat as he arrived at a more accurate guess.

If he had been himself, he definitely would've pretended not to see Sothoth Yann leap from the building to intercept him. He would've fled before the man could say a word and kept running until he shook off his pursuer, much like the Admiral of Stars fleeing the knowledge that pursued her.

Unfortunately, I'm currently Gehrman Sparrow. A crazy adventurer wouldn't flee over such a trivial matter... Sigh, getting to know myself better is an added benefit... Klein stopped and watched Sothoth land firmly on the ground before bowing with a hand over his chest.

"Mr. Gehrman Sparrow, we have a very important task to entrust you."

Here it comes... Klein calmly replied, "I won't have much free time in the near future."

"It won't take much of your time. Hear what it involves before deciding whether to accept or refuse," Sothoth said earnestly.

I'm afraid I'll have no choice but to accept once I've heard it... Eh, actually, that's fine. If Bilt Brando and Sothoth Yann threaten me into accepting the mission, I don't mind turning them into gold pounds and Beyonder ingredients... Klein considered the matter carefully and realized he had little need for restraint at sea. Possessing the strength of a pirate admiral made him virtually unstoppable in most places.

He took out his pocket watch and flipped it open.

"I'm giving you five minutes."

"Alright, that's enough." Sothoth pointed at the entrance to the Sweet Lemon Bar behind Klein.

Back in the room, Klein glanced at the wall clock and said coldly, "You have three minutes and twelve seconds left."

His attitude gave Bilt an inexplicable sense of reassurance. He began to believe Gehrman Sparrow truly had the ability to complete the mission.

"Mr. Gehrman, I heard you can transform into anyone, just as Vice Admiral Hurricane Qilangos once could," Bilt said hurriedly.

Who revealed that? Klein nearly raised his eyebrows, and his gaze darkened as it settled on Bilt.

If news that Gehrman Sparrow could transform into anyone spread, it would cause Klein considerable trouble. Others would either think of Creeping Hunger and connect him to Qilangos's death, or suspect that he was a Faceless Beyonder harboring certain secrets. The former would attract the Twilight Hermit Order's attention, while the latter would prompt the Church of the Evernight Goddess to send personnel to investigate.

Of course, the Church of the Evernight Goddess wasn't a powerful force at sea, so Klein didn't need to worry too much about it. He just didn't wish to be in conflict with the Nighthawks.

If that happened, he would have no choice but to abandon his identity as Gehrman Sparrow. This came at the critical point when more and more people were learning of the crazy, powerful adventurer with a soft heart, allowing Klein to receive recognition and feedback.

Bilt sensed the change in Gehrman Sparrow's gaze and tensed. He chuckled. "The news came from the Black Death."

Tracy... This is her revenge. One day, I'll hunt her down... Klein nodded slightly. "You may consider it true."

He didn't explain how he received his powers of shapeshifting, as this didn't match Gehrman Sparrow's persona.

Bilt sighed in relief. After two seconds of silence, he said, "We're helping an important figure find a stand-in."

Under Gehrman Sparrow's inscrutable gaze, he explained in detail, "That important figure needs to handle a secret matter alone and doesn't want anyone else to know. Therefore, he needs a stand-in to represent him in his daily affairs, meeting various guests and spending time with his mistress without arousing suspicion. He will return in three to five days.

"We had already found someone who resembled him and spent a great deal of time correcting his posture and accent. We even reported the good news to the important figure. Who could've known that the tramp would overeat and suddenly die last night?

"That important figure is about to begin his operation. We do not have the time to look for another person who looks like him, so we can only seek your help."

Isn't this the genuine acting opportunity I've been looking for... Klein was tempted.

However, one point immediately caught his attention.

For Bilt, the leader of the Adventurer Association, to call someone an important figure meant the person was important indeed. Nothing surrounding such a person would be simple. The mission carried considerable risk!

Klein considered it briefly before asking in a deep voice, "Do you think this will be dangerous?"

"No. As long as you aren't exposed, there shouldn't be any danger. You need to represent the important figure at social engagements. Heh heh, no one would dare assassinate him. Yes... I know what you're worried about. You're afraid this important figure will silence everyone who knows about the matter once it's over? Don't worry. Pirates have fabricated plenty of vicious rumors about him, so even if the mission succeeds, no one will believe you if you divulge it in the future. It will merely be treated as a joke," Bilt said with a smile, spreading his hands.

Klein dropped the subject and asked instead, "What are you offering?"

"1,000 pounds and three opportunities to have the Artisan make something for you. Heh heh, I'll cover every expense other than the materials." Based on Gehrman Sparrow's previous commission, Bilt believed he would find the offer difficult to resist.

It's not bad... Besides, it gives me a chance to do true acting...

Klein took a look at the wall clock and said, "I'll return and consider. I'll give you the answer in the evening."

Regardless, he had to go above the gray fog and divine the level of danger!

Considering that Bilt and Sothoth were Sequence 7 or perhaps even Sequence 6 Beyonders, Klein didn't excuse himself to use the washroom, lest they sense that something was amiss.

As the important figure's identity hadn't been revealed, Bilt wasn't too worried. He nodded.

"Alright.

"I hope for a pleasant partnership."

Williams Street. A stout man from Feysac who was nearly two meters tall was searching for clues.

Suddenly, he felt his spiritual perception trigger as he looked up.

Light resembling the morning sun's rays surfaced around his body, illuminating the surrounding houses and streetlamps as though they were illusions.

At the same time, sparks soared on the street as all kinds of supernatural phenomena appeared.

Indeed, the Sauron family has sent people... His suspicions confirmed, the Feysacian man looked straight down the street.

He saw Nighthawks wearing red or black gloves as black gun barrels covered with complicated patterns extended out of the rooftops. They were aimed at him while the surrounding passersby didn't seem to notice anything amiss.

He also saw Horamick, archbishop of the Church of Steam and Machinery's Backlund diocese, and Saint Anthony, archbishop of the Church of the Evernight Goddess's Backlund diocese.

Immediately afterward, he caught sight of members of the Sauron family raising their hands above their heads from the corner of his eye.

...The powerful Feysac spies did the same.

They had decisively chosen to surrender!

Resistance only spelled certain death. There was still a chance of being repatriated by surrendering!

At half past six that evening, Klein returned to the Sweet Lemon Bar and met Bilt and Sothoth.

He expressionlessly said, "An advance of 500 pounds.

"Then you'll have to tell me whom I'm supposed to become."

Bilt couldn't hold back his smile as he gestured for Sothoth to collect the funds from the safe while saying with a deep voice, "He's the kingdom's highest-ranking navy commander of the Central Sonia Sea.

"His Excellency Admiral Amyrius Rieveldt!

"A real demigod!"

Chapter 621: Governor-General's Office Banquet

Admiral Amyrius Rieveldt... A true demigod... Upon hearing Bilt's revelation, several thoughts immediately flashed through Klein's mind.

I'm sorry. Goodbye!

Gehrman Sparrow raised his eyebrows slightly as Bilt hurriedly explained, "This won't make the mission any more difficult.

"There's no need for anyone to display a demigod's powers throughout the duration of the mission."

He cleared his throat and forced a smile.

"To make the mission easier, His Excellency has made special arrangements to inspect the Oravi naval base for a few days. This way, he won't need to remain at the headquarters of the Central Sonia Sea navy in the City of Generosity, Bayam. As such, he will avoid Sea King Jahn Kottman and the governor-general of the Rorsted Archipelago, George Negan. He will also avoid most of the subordinates he knows, his family members who manage the family estate there, and the mistress who knows him best.

"In other words, you won't need to face any demigods or withstand the scrutiny of a crowd.

"Here, only three people are familiar with His Excellency. The first is his secretary, Lieutenant Colonel Luan. He belongs to MI9 and is responsible for monitoring His Excellency. He likely uses many names, and I'm not sure which one is real. The second is His Excellency's local mistress, a beautiful lady named Cynthia. It's said that one of her ancestors was a noble whose title was stripped before the family was exiled here. The third is Oravi's governor-general, Aston Rieveldt, His Excellency's youngest brother. Earl Rieveldt of the House of Lords is their elder brother."

It really doesn't sound too difficult. Besides, my divination results tell me that it's not too dangerous... Klein fell silent for a few seconds before nodding gently.

"I need the detailed information regarding Amyrius Rieveldt."

"We've already prepared everything. This is his photograph. These are descriptions of the distinctive features concealed beneath his clothing, the peculiarities of his accent, the words he commonly uses, and his reactions and attitudes toward different matters. Here is detailed information on how he interacts with Luan, Cynthia, and Aston..." Delighted, Bilt produced all the information he had received from Admiral Amyrius Rieveldt.

Klein first took the photograph and saw a middle-aged gentleman with black hair and blue eyes. He had a strict and old-fashioned demeanor, and his hair was a lot more voluminous than most Loen men.

He nodded indiscernibly before looking up.

"I know this is a lot of information, but I believe you can memorize it all in two days. You must be very experienced in such matters..." Before Bilt could finish, he instinctively took a step back, because the person before him was no longer Gehrman Sparrow but Amyrius Rieveldt! The stern and arrogant air he exuded was no different from that of the real man!

"Holy Lord of Storms, th-this is practically a miracle!" Bilt looked him up and down in amazement. "However, you could be another three centimeters taller, and your legs could be thicker. But that's fine. There's no hurry. His Excellency will arrive the day after tomorrow with the Imperial Navy's First Central Sonia Fleet. He will inspect the Oravi naval base in the morning and attend a banquet at the governor-general's office. I have an invitation and can bring you along. You can observe His Excellency's behavior and the way he interacts with different people at close range."

As he spoke, he received the 500 pounds that Sothoth had taken out from the safe. He handed it to Klein and said, "I hope for a pleasant partnership!"

Klein weighed the notes in his hand and studied them with a few glances before saying, "I hope for a pleasant partnership."

Backlund, Williams Street.

As an inconspicuous member of the Red Gloves, Leonard Mitchell leaned on the outer wall of a house, waiting for the preliminary investigation to end.

He had his right leg raised slightly, with only the toes touching the ground. It gave him a languid air.

After a while, he saw his teammate return with a complicated expression, at once excited, puzzled, expectant, and nervous.

"Thomson, is there a conclusion?" Leonard's heart stirred as he leaned over with a smile.

"Yeah." Thomson nodded and answered frankly, "Both sides told the truth. They had no way to lie in their dreams."

Thomson, whose hair was thinning, put his top hat back on.

"Their accounts are consistent. Beneath this street is a ruin belonging to the Fourth Epoch's Tudor Dynasty. The entrance is indeed that abandoned chapel. No one knows if there's another entrance for now."

"Is that so..." Leonard didn't delve into the details as he exchanged a few words before moving to the sidelines. Once again, he leaned against the wall.

He looked around and lowered his voice.

"Old Man, this is what you're good at.

"There's a Tudor family ruin hidden here."

An elderly voice rang out in his mind.

"You're becoming more and more impolite. In our era, disrespecting a person of high standing meant being made into a living sacrifice.

"Also, it's impossible for there to only be one Tudor family ruin in Backlund."

"Are they lying?" Leonard asked softly.

The elderly voice chuckled and said, "No, they just don't know enough.

"If my theory is correct, these ruins likely date back to the Tudor-Trunsoest United Empire."

"What?" Leonard was stunned.

This was an ancient dynasty he had never heard of before.

The slightly-aged voice chuckled.

"What an ignorant child. After the Solomon Empire was first destroyed, it was replaced by the Tudor-Trunsoest United Empire. Its two consuls ruled the entire Northern Continent."

"Double consul..." Leonard ruminated over this phrase.

The slightly-aged voice chuckled and sighed.

"In the underground ruins, there should be 41 inverted candlesticks on the left and 40 on the right. Together, they resemble a giant's throne, and... Heh heh. It might be where Alista Tudor became the Blood Emperor."

Leonard frowned slightly before relaxing his brow. He said with a relaxed smile, "There must be quite a lot of secrets hidden in there."

"Of course, but you aren't qualified to know about them." The slightly aged voice clicked his tongue.

Leonard curled his lips indiscernibly and said, "Next comes the exploration of the ruins."

The slightly-aged voice chuckled before falling silent.

A minute later, Leonard saw Saint Anthony and Archbishop Horamick of the Church of Steam end their conversation and return to their respective camps.

Immediately afterward, Horamick ordered all Machinery Hivemind personnel to leave, yielding the area to the Church of the Evernight Goddess's Nighthawks.

What happened? Upon seeing this scene, Leonard was puzzled.

At that moment, all the Nighthawks heard Archbishop Saint Anthony's voice.

"All Red Gloves gather. The other Nighthawks are to leave the mirror world. Find a reason to evacuate all the residents in the area, and promise them compensation for their property.

"After all the residents evacuate, the Red Gloves and I will destroy this ruin that stems from an evil era!

"Entry is forbidden. It will be directly destroyed!

"May the Goddess bless us all."

This... Leonard never expected such a development.

At seven-thirty in the evening, at the Oravi governor-general's office.

Klein assumed Sothoth's appearance. Wearing a black tailcoat and a bow tie of the same color, he followed Bilt into the banquet hall.

The temperature inside was like that of spring. A gigantic chandelier hung from the ceiling, and the shimmering candlelight illuminated the hall as brightly as day.

In the right-hand corner, musicians dressed in vests and bow ties were playing a brisk tune. Long tables stood on the left, laden with roasted chicken, pan-fried foie gras, stewed lamb, Backlund-style roasted goose, Odora lobsters with butter and cheese, and other delicacies.

Despite the distance, Klein could still catch a whiff of the fragrance wafting over. He planned on getting a plate and filling it with food.

At that moment, Bilt tugged self-consciously at his collar. He leaned over and lowered his voice.

"Remember the etiquette expected at such banquets.

"Our current goal is to observe His Excellency's behavior, so just a glass of wine will do.

"There's misty champagne here, Aurmir grape wine, and Southville red wine, all celebrated wines that are seldom seen outside. You can drink some, but not a lot. We have to maintain sufficient sobriety. Yes, try to just go through the motions when drinking."

Klein looked away and nodded.

The two of them each took a glass of golden champagne, its tiny bubbles resembling mist, from a red-vested waiter passing by. Then they moved toward the center of the banquet, where Amyrius Rieveldt stood in his dark blue naval admiral's uniform.

Given their status, they naturally had no way of approaching the admiral. All they could do was observe his every action from a short distance.

Amyrius's figure is normal. It doesn't look that fit. The corners of his mouth droop a little, indicating his actual age...

He doesn't have a beard. His blue eyes seem to hide an authority that one cannot reject or defend against...

His dark blue admiral's uniform is crisply starched. There's a red lanyard at his shoulder, which joins to his chest where there are all sorts of medals...

His sleeves have golden cuff links, accentuating his epaulets of the same color...

His epaulets are divided into three sections. From the inside out, there's a ruby-inlaid crown, a crossed scepter and sword, and four diamond stars... Klein scrutinized the naval admiral, using his Faceless powers to memorize every detail of his appearance as well as the manner he adopted when speaking to different people.

During this process, he only took a sip of the misty champagne and didn't take note of its taste.

After he had mostly gathered all the information, Klein exhaled and allowed his mind to rest.

The mental exertion made him hungry. Thus, he placed his wineglass on the tray of a red-vested waiter and prepared to get some food from the long table.

At this moment, Bilt leaned over and said, "His Excellency has given me the signal. We will be meeting him at an agreed location."

Klein tore his gaze away from the roasted chicken and swept a cold glance at Bilt.

Bilt shuddered as he hurriedly turned around, leading Gehrman Sparrow through the floor-to-ceiling window and into the garden.

After walking for nearly a minute along the secluded path, he paused and pointed at Klein's face.

"You can change into His Excellency's appearance.

"Just pretend that you're that tramp who looks like him."

Klein nodded gently and extended his palm to wipe his face.

He did so to conceal the disgusting tendrils and granules that might appear on his face while changing his appearance.

By the time he lowered his palm, Bilt saw a middle-aged gentleman who looked somewhat like Amyrius Rieveldt.

"Very good." Bilt took a few steps forward and came to the door to a storeroom. He extended his hand and pushed open the unlocked door.

Amyrius Rieveldt was still wearing the eye-catching admiral's uniform. He stood with his side to the door, gazing out at the garden through the window.

At this moment, he turned his head and fixed his gaze on the person who resembled him.

The indescribable pressure and might made Klein involuntarily lower his head.

Amyrius Rieveldt's gaze didn't linger on him but slowly shifted to Bilt. Without a trace of emotion, he said, "This isn't the tramp you mentioned before.

"He's a Beyonder."

Chapter 622: Temporary Contract

Upon hearing Amyrius Rieveldt's declaration, Bilt's forehead instantly broke out into a cold sweat.

His mouth fell open as he tried to explain himself, but in the end, he dropped to his knees and said under the indescribable pressure, "Admiral, Your Excellency, the tramp from before suddenly died of an illness. I had no choice but to find an adventurer who can shapeshift."

At that moment, Klein wasn't too nervous. Amyrius Rieveldt had already noticed him during the banquet; there was no reason he would only now recognize him as a Beyonder. The fact that he had agreed to meet them instead of avoiding the risk altogether meant he didn't mind whom Bilt had hired.

Not worried at all? Is this the confidence of a demigod, or does his Beyonder pathway allow him to foresee and avert danger...? With great difficulty, Klein raised his head and looked at the naval admiral beside him.

"Quite strong," Amyrius Rieveldt commented without an expression.

He continued looking at the genuflecting Bilt and said, "Do not try to use your petty tricks in front of me.

"An ordinary person and a Beyonder in this world are different, and I'm a follower of order, so I can be certain of that."

Of course, it's exactly as the information described. This admiral has a tendency to expound on certain matters. I have to remember that. His style is completely different from mine and Gehrman Sparrow's... Lost in thought, Klein lowered his gaze to the ground as though unable to withstand the pressure.

Amyrius Rieveldt took one step forward.

"Lying is your first mistake. Carelessness is your second.

"A tramp you spent a great deal of effort grooming suddenly dies, and then an adventurer who can shapeshift appears before you. Don't you find that too coincidental?"

Yes, it's quite coincidental... Klein nearly said the same thing.

If not for his confirmation above the gray fog, he would've suspected that he was being set up by a legendary creature or a Grade 0 Sealed Artifact.

Bilt's pupils constricted as he snapped to his senses.

He realized that, overcome by terror, he had thought only of grasping at one final life-saving straw. He had lost the caution born of experience and failed to consider whether Gehrman Sparrow's appearance was truly a coincidence.

The tramp suddenly died on the day he arrived at the Sweet Lemon Bar! The more Bilt thought about it, the more he felt as though he had fallen for some intricately planned trap.

As Amyrius Rieveldt watched Bilt's expression change with enlightenment and regret, he nodded gently and said, "My father, the deceased Earl Rieveldt, once taught me this.

"He said to pardon the first mistake of a subordinate.

"Bilt, you should be thankful for his benevolence."

Bilt's tense mood eased instantly as he felt extremely moved.

He had thought Amyrius Rieveldt, who was closer to a god than a man, would execute him on the spot as a warning to all the adventurers under him. He never imagined the admiral would choose to pardon him.

"Your Excellency, I... I..." Bilt was momentarily unable to form a sentence.

Maintaining his stern expression, Amyrius said in a deep voice, "The saying has a second half: 'Punish them for their second mistake.' Bilt, do you know what you should do from now on?"

The kneeling Bilt immediately straightened his back and pressed his right fist to his left chest.

"I will be utterly loyal to you, Your Excellency!"

Amyrius nodded and turned to Klein.

"What's your name?"

That depends on which identity you're asking about... Klein lampooned inwardly and calmly replied, "Gehrman Sparrow."

Amyrius Rieveldt suddenly fell silent for two seconds as the atmosphere in the storeroom seemed to freeze.

Just as Klein couldn't help but feel uneasy, Amyrius finally said, "So it's you."

Your Excellency, you sound as though you know me. I'm just an ordinary military informant. I only exchanged some bounty through you. I haven't even made any reimbursement claims... Klein silently mumbled as he felt less and less confident.

Amyrius nodded and said to Bilt and Klein, "The plan will proceed as per normal.

"However, we need to sign a contract."

A contract? Klein resisted the pressure with great difficulty as he raised his head to look at Amyrius.

Amyrius offered no further explanation. He picked up the paper and pen he had prepared on the windowsill and began writing.

With every stroke of his pen, a golden glow shone forth. Its solemnity and holiness made it seem as though he were inscribing a law.

Klein narrowed his eyes slowly as his vision turned blurry. He couldn't help but lower his head again.

After an unknown period of time, Amyrius stopped writing and picked up a piece of paper. He said to Klein, "Sign your name at the end.

"If the conditions aren't satisfactory, you can choose not to sign."

Do you think I have or lack the guts to sign it? Klein lampooned as he watched Bilt stand up. He received the pen and paper and handed it to Klein.

The terms on the paper were few and simple. Most restricted Gehrman Sparrow's actions while impersonating Amyrius Rieveldt. They included, but were not limited to, deliberately revealing flaws, using Amyrius's identity to engage in acts detrimental to him, and having intimate contact with Miss Cynthia.

I thought such important figures wouldn't care about the chastity of their mistresses... This admiral is indeed an old-fashioned man... However, I'm not such a person either... Klein hid his curiosity and asked as though it was an academic question, "What if Miss Cynthia chooses to make intimate contact with me?"

His underlying point was that acting too distant or resistant would make it easier for Cynthia to notice something amiss. He was asking how to strike the proper balance.

"It won't be a problem," Amyrius said expressionlessly. "For the duration of the contract, you will either feel no desire for her or be incapable of acting on it."

You can do that? This contract is quite powerful... Aside from contracts with spirit world creatures, this is the first time I've seen another kind of contract. Furthermore, those contracts used the power of the Underworld, while this one is strictly between two parties... Is this one of Admiral Amyrius's demigod-level Beyonder powers? The Arbiter pathway? Klein glanced at the dates and discovered that the contract lasted five days.

Is he confident that he'll return within five days, or can he only enforce a contract for five days at his level? Klein read the terms once more before taking the fountain pen and writing the name Gehrman Sparrow.

With the final character written, he saw the words on the piece of paper emit a golden glow as they condensed into a resplendent luster.

Amidst the resplendent luster, the piece of paper rapidly dissipated and vanished, as though it had fused with the world's rules.

An invisible and faint gray fog gently rippled around him as Klein clearly sensed layers of indescribable restrictions placed on him.

These restrictions quickly melted into his body, temporarily becoming one with his Spirit Body and physical flesh.

The gray fog can block out bad and good luck from external sources to a certain extent, but it's unable to screen a contract I signed... That's right. If it can screen it, the contract with the messenger wouldn't have been valid... Klein gazed at Amyrius Rieveldt once again in enlightenment.

The admiral had already converged his might as he produced a dark gold charm in his palm.

On it were the symbols and magical markings representing the "Sword of Judgment." Its design was restrained and solemn, giving the impression that it was part of a codex.

"This is a high-level charm made using my blood, a Fourth Epoch ancient codex, and the royal family's Grade 0 Sealed Artifact," Amyrius said calmly. "Its name is Ninth Law.
