By infusing it with a minute amount of spirituality, even an ordinary person can produce an aura similar to mine. Without it, you would find it very difficult to impersonate me."

Indeed, the imposing aura I produce while disguised is only superficial. It's usually enough to fool others, but it would be useless if I needed to overawe a subordinate... Klein secretly heaved a sigh of relief.

Amyrius continued, "With your present strength, you should be able to withstand its usage.

"Through it, you can indicate a necessary restriction on the target, placing them in an extremely disadvantageous state.

"This way, even if a demigod were to sound you out, you'll be able to scare him away.

"If no mishaps happen at the end of everything, and if you have no need to use it, then it will be yours to keep.

"It can be used for a year."

Klein was first taken aback before he felt a strong sense of delight.

For the first time since using the charm created with the Mutated Sun Sacred Emblem, he finally possessed another high-level charm.

Although such an item had a limited lifespan and could only be used once, it had the advantage of not causing any negative side effects. Of course, the stringent requirements for creating one made such charms extremely rare.

As expected of a demigod. He's far more generous than Bilt... The cost of bribing a tramp is entirely different from that of hiring an adventurer at the level of a pirate admiral... Delighted, Klein silently extended his palm and accepted the high-level charm named Ninth Law.

Then, he saw Amyrius remove his belt.

After a brief silence, Klein kept his embarrassment in control as he took off his clothes without an expression on his face.

Soon, he had switched clothes with Amyrius and had donned the dark blue starched admiral's uniform.

After watching Amyrius and Bilt walk off through a secluded path in the garden, Klein calmly adjusted his buttons and turned to look at the glass window.

Under the dark night and the crimson moonlight, the glass window was like a mirror. It vaguely reflected Klein's present looks.

He had neatly combed-back black hair, profound blue eyes, slightly drooping cheeks, a clean-shaven face, and a stern, old-fashioned demeanor. He was dressed in a dark blue uniform adorned with a lanyard, medals, and epaulets.

Klein twitched the corners of his mouth as he silently said to himself, From this moment forth, I'm a naval admiral.

Chapter 623: First Night

As he touched the Ninth Law charm in his pocket, Klein adjusted his height and details regarding his appearance, making him look identical to Amyrius Rieveldt.

He left the storeroom through another door and followed a silent corridor back into the governor-general's office.

Along the way, waiters and maidservants occasionally passed him, but no one dared look directly at him. The mere sight of his admiral's uniform sent them scurrying to the sides, where they bowed with lowered heads.

Anyone of the same height could probably make it into the banquet hall while wearing these clothes... I have to admit, acting as an important figure can be easier than acting as an ordinary person... Looking straight ahead and maintaining his solemn demeanor, Klein walked unhurriedly toward a path paved with black bricks.

He soon heard beautiful, melodious music. Elegant gas wall lamps illuminated the dark surroundings.

Just as Klein approached a lounge, its door opened and a middle-aged man who had been waiting inside walked over.

The man had black hair and blue eyes. His features somewhat resembled Amyrius's, but his forehead was higher, the bags beneath his eyes were puffy, and the corners of his mouth didn't droop.

He was none other than Amyrius Rieveldt's youngest brother, Aston Rieveldt.

This gentleman had once served in the navy and had been promoted to colonel after rendering meritorious service in the Southern Continent's colonies. Later, weary of his military career and influenced by political considerations, he changed careers and became governor-general.

During his five or six years in Oravi, recognizing the importance of the island's location and resources, he had encouraged the Rieveldt family to purchase large amounts of farmland and numerous estates, leaving him with considerable property holdings.

Nor had he acquired it solely through his authority. Aston and the Rieveldt family had paid a fair price and even taken out bank loans. It wasn't like the eastern coast of Balam, where land belonging to the Feysac people had been forcibly purchased at extremely low prices.

Of course, if he hadn't been Oravi's governor-general and his elder brother hadn't been the highest commander of the Central Sonia Sea navy, the Rieveldt family couldn't have so easily persuaded the owners to sell such excellent farmland and estates.

Here comes the test... Klein calmly approached and stopped in front of Aston Rieveldt.

Aston looked around and asked in a deep voice, "Have you decided on that matter?"

What matter...? Klein's mind initially went blank before he recalled a note in the information he had received: "If Aston requests a private conversation or asks for an answer regarding a certain matter, tell him that he will receive an answer upon your departure from Oravi."

Admiral Amyrius really anticipated this. I just need to watch my attitude and tone. Yes, I also need to use the unique terms and pronunciations used by Loen aristocrats... Klein gently nodded and sternly said, "Wait a few more days.

"You'll receive your answer when I leave Oravi."

Aston didn't raise any doubts as he chuckled.

"You seem to be waiting for something to give you the strength to decide."

Does it have to do with the matter Admiral Amyrius must handle personally? Klein's heart stirred as he adopted the familiar tone of a person in authority.

"Keep your conjectures to yourself."

With that, he stepped forward and walked toward the banquet hall.

Aston Rieveldt focused on his elder brother's back, his expression gradually turning cold. He then shook his head slightly.

After entering the banquet hall, Klein surveyed the area and walked toward the long table laden with food. From time to time, he stopped to exchange pleasantries with people who approached him.

In the process, he realized that he didn't need to understand the topics raised in conversation. He only needed to nod occasionally, allowing each exchange to proceed smoothly to its conclusion.

Indeed, the status of an important figure makes certain aspects of acting easy, but correspondingly, there are certain matters that can be difficult... Klein tore through one "obstacle" after another before finally arriving at the long table.

He casually picked up a plate, reminding himself that Admiral Amyrius liked fish, beef, and lobster but disliked chicken and goose. Therefore, he avoided the roasted chicken and Backlund-style roasted goose, instead taking some beef, fried Dragon-Bone Fish, and Odora lobster with butter and cheese.

With the metal containers insulated by asbestos and heated from below by red-hot charcoal or steaming water, all the food remained at a suitable temperature. Klein nearly lost control of himself and broke character when he took his first bite.

He did his best to maintain Admiral Amyrius's image as he held his plate and chatted with the port city's member of parliament, naval suppliers, and others. He listened solemnly to what they had to say, taking a bite from time to time.

He noticed that a young man in a tailcoat kept following behind him.

His neat blond hair was combed back from a receding hairline. With light-blue eyes, he looked handsome and gentlemanly.

Identical to the picture. Amyrius's secretary, Luan... Klein controlled himself and didn't size him up. He wanted to fill his stomach before the end of the banquet.

Upon leaving the governor-general's office, Klein boarded the carriage guarded by his bodyguards and sat beside the wine cabinet.

The blond secretary, Luan, followed him inside. His leather boots sank into the thick, soft carpet as he silently moved to a spot opposite Klein.

He sat there, but he only occupied a third of the seat.

The carriage began moving as Luan took out a stack of documents from the black briefcase he carried.

"Your Excellency, this is the Oravi naval base's ledger for the year 1349."

Klein extended his hand over and casually browsed through a few pages.

What? A pound for a roll of toilet paper? The naval base's bathroom was renovated twenty times in a single year? Klein performed some simple calculations and discovered all sorts of absurd line items.

Isn't this accounting far too crude and obvious? I could teach them a whole course on how to pad expense claims! Klein seriously considered what attitude he should adopt.

From his point of view, the greatest difficulty in disguising as Amyrius was to fool Luan.

This wasn't to say that Governor-General Aston and Miss Cynthia were less familiar with Admiral Amyrius than his secretary. Rather, as his younger brother, Aston might help conceal the matter if he noticed something amiss after receiving a hint. Similarly, as his mistress and dependent, Cynthia would be inclined to help the admiral conceal it.

Of course, the possibility that Cynthia was a spy couldn't be eliminated. There was still the matter of there being a chance of being used by a spy, but nothing could be as dangerous as Luan, whose duty was to monitor the admiral.

I can't expose any problems... What kind of attitude would Admiral Amyrius have when faced with such a report? Go into a rage, or pretend to be in a rage? No, the Oravi naval base personnel wouldn't be that daring to hand over a clearly problematic report as though he were blind. They must have a certain level of confidence and a tacit understanding between them... As the information didn't mention this, Klein could only make a judgment based on his experience.

Furthermore, he could confirm that either the Oravi naval base's report was beyond Admiral Amyrius's expectations or was determined to be of little importance. Just based on the schedule, it was something that could easily be dealt with.

Regardless of the possibilities, I have to adopt the attitude typically employed by someone of high standing: reveal nothing of my position... Klein closed the documents and handed them back to his blond secretary, Luan. He said expressionlessly, "Put them on my desk."

The implication was, "I'll look through them carefully." To different people, however, it would mean different things depending on their positions.

If the Oravi naval base had done it without warning, this meant that the admiral was somewhat dissatisfied and was awaiting an explanation.

If the two sides had reached a tacit understanding on the matter, it meant that Admiral Amyrius wanted a more favorable share of the benefits. Klein didn't care whether this offended anyone. After all, he wouldn't be Amyrius Rieveldt in a few days' time. He believed that a true demigod had the means to suppress his subordinates' anger.

In addition, he was grateful that Amyrius wasn't a demigod of the Church of Storms. Otherwise, he would have had to decide whether to nod and approve the report or hurl the document back in anger while tossing a few people into the sea to feed the fish for good measure.

"Yes, Your Excellency." The blond secretary, Luan, didn't change his expression. He stuffed the document back into his black briefcase as though he had long expected such a response.

On the way back, Klein reclined in the carriage in accordance with Amyrius Rieveldt's habits. He half-closed his eyes as though contemplating important matters, but in fact, he wasn't thinking about anything.

Luan maintained his silence and didn't speak at all.

Human-height iron-black street lamps were quickly left behind as the carriage drove close to the naval base before taking a bend into a house with a garden and lawn.

Just as Klein walked up the steps, a butler opened the door for him as servants lined the two sides, reverently awaiting his entrance.

The living room was decorated in a classical style. Oil paintings of beautiful scenery hung on the walls, complemented by limestone statues and simple, elegant vases. A faint but lingering fragrance permeated the room and soothed the heart.

Klein, who should have relaxed, instead tensed when he saw a beautiful lady in a house dress walking toward him.

She looked to be in her early twenties. Her blonde hair cascaded down, and when she cast her blue eyes over, it was as though there was a glow hidden within them. While filled with gentle femininity, there were still some remnants of her adolescence. She was none other than Admiral Amyrius's mistress, Cynthia.

Klein held back his discomfort and allowed Amyrius's stern face to reveal a smile as he widened his arms.

Cynthia threw herself into his arms and stood on tiptoe. Pressing her cheek to his, she whispered with a smile, "Admiral, I've already drawn a hot bath for you."

This means she had someone watching for the banquet to end... Being a mistress isn't simple either... Admiral Amyrius does enjoy hot baths to clear his mind... To ignore the closeness of their cheeks, Klein allowed his thoughts to wander.

As a straight man, he should've felt embarrassed while also being rather glad to be approached by such a beautiful member of the opposite sex. However, the temporary contract made him have zero urges. Hence, all that was left was awkwardness.

"Very good," Klein praised her as he gently pushed Cynthia away, his hands having had nowhere else to go.

Knowing that the admiral didn't like to be intimate in front of the servants, Cynthia retreated and led Klein to the second floor. He was brought into the bathroom as he prepared a bathrobe for him.

After doing all of this, Cynthia instructed the servants not to come to the second floor unless they heard the bell ring. Then, she returned to the bedroom, took off all her clothes and changed into a nightgown made of silk.

Her nightgown revealed plenty of her chest. It was an alluring snow-white, and deep in her cleavage was a special necklace pendant. It was like a miniaturized black rhinoceros horn about the length of a finger segment.

Cynthia took off the necklace and stuffed it under her pillow. Amidst her blushing and hesitation, she left the bedroom and came outside the bathroom where the admiral was bathing. Mustering her strength, she pulled at the handle.

Creak. Her hand paused. She realized that the bathroom door had been locked from the inside at some point in time.

With a blank look, Cynthia instinctively yanked at it again.

Creak. Creak. The bathroom's door didn't budge an inch.

Chapter 624: Warning

The bathroom had a partition. The emanating steam covered the entire bathtub.

Apart from his head, Klein's entire body was soaked in the hot water. He lay there in such great comfort that he didn't even wish to move his toes.

What a beautiful night... If only there was no Cynthia outside. I still have to deal with her later... Klein sighed as he considered what excuse to use to avoid being intimate with her.

In keeping with Admiral Amyrius Rieveldt's character, he decided to use work as his first excuse. After that, he could claim to feel unwell, to have lost the ability to have sex, to need time to absorb some medicine he had just taken, or to have suddenly discovered his true sexual orientation and developed an intense attraction to curly-haired baboons.

As for whether this would damage the admiral's image, Klein didn't feel the slightest bit of pressure. As long as he prevented Cynthia from suspecting that he was a fake Amyrius Rieveldt, he would've perfectly completed his mission.

Explaining those excuses afterward, along with his eventual recovery, would be Admiral Amyrius's problem. What does that have to do with me, Gehrman Sparrow? And what does Gehrman Sparrow's ruined reputation have to do with me, Sherlock Moriarty? Satisfied, Klein stood and stepped dripping wet into the dry partition. He took a bath towel and dried himself.

After putting on a bathrobe hanging nearby, Klein silently drew a breath as though he were about to face a formidable enemy, then opened the bathroom door.

Seeing the corridor empty, with only the light from the wall lamps on both sides illuminating the darkness, Klein felt slightly relieved and was no longer as tense.

This feels almost the same as challenging a pirate admiral... As he muttered to himself, he realized there was a serious problem. He had no idea which rooms were the master bedroom and the study.

Regardless, I have to inform Cynthia; otherwise, it would make Admiral Amyrius appear strange... Klein recalled the layout of similar houses in an attempt to accurately find the master bedroom.

At this moment, the door to the room diagonally across him creaked open. Cynthia, who was draped in a silk nightgown, walked over.

Her blonde hair cascaded down in damp disarray, a few strands hanging before her blue eyes and bright-red lips. They partially concealed the light in her eyes, while her lips were slightly parted. She appeared extremely alluring in the dim light.

The silk nightgown had a plunging neckline, revealing snow-white skin and deep cleavage to Klein's gaze.

Klein nearly raised his head to look at the ceiling to avoid the intense stimulation.

Calm down, calm down. You are Amyrius Rieveldt... Besides, you've seen a half-naked Demoness before... Compared to an Ailment Maiden, this lady's charms are clearly insufficient. Wait, why am I thinking about Demonesses? Who knows if they were formerly men or women... Klein maintained his gaze as he sized her up and down with a "teasing" tone.

In that instant, he could sense that primal desire, but there was no response from his nether regions...

This is the result of the temporary contract? In fact, Admiral Amyrius didn't need to go through the trouble. Even if I have the urge, I'll be able to control myself... What kind of situation haven't I encountered before?

Yes... Cynthia is a little different from what the records describe. Didn't she become Amyrius's mistress one or two years ago? Since they spend most of their time apart, doesn't the admiral usually have to coax her because her pent-up desires haven't been satisfied? Why is she the one taking the initiative today?

Does she find her standing as his mistress unstable, or has she been switched? Ever since Klein became a Faceless, he felt that everyone was a fake.

Cynthia lowered her eyelids. The courage she had mustered ebbed away beneath Amyrius's scrutiny. A deep blush spread across her face; she was embarrassed but also somewhat proud.

Then, she heard him calmly instruct, "Help me prepare some coffee and place it in my study.

"I have many things to deal with tonight. You don't have to wait for me."

Cynthia raised her head suddenly, her embarrassed blushing still present.

She had momentarily failed to understand what Admiral Amyrius had said.

Klein secretly inhaled and went forward to hug her before kissing her gently on the forehead.

"I'll spend plenty of time with you in a few days."

Such a response came from the provided information, but he had slightly changed the terms.

To be frank, without some prior understanding, Klein would have believed that Admiral Amyrius wore the same stoic expression while spending time with his mistress, even during sex. He looked stern in everything he said and did, but this demigod also had a gentle side to his speech. He simply wasn't very good at sweet nothings.

This also made him understand something else: a person's outward appearance rarely revealed what they were like in private. For a Faceless to achieve a convincing disguise, they had to investigate meticulously and thoroughly understand the target. Just as a Magician never performed unprepared, neither could a Faceless.

Cynthia revealed a clear look of disappointment, but she quickly restrained those feelings as she smiled.

"Alright.

"Admiral, your nightgown is in the room. A bathrobe isn't suitable for handling matters."

This matches the information exactly. She's quite considerate and understanding... Klein watched Cynthia turn and enter the room before pulling the bell cord. He took the opportunity to remove his bathrobe and change into a dark-red nightshirt with trousers of the same color.

Cynthia thoughtfully opened the study door and tidied the slightly cluttered desk. Then she waited for the maidservant to finish making the coffee before personally carrying it in.

Meanwhile, Klein read the documents and reports with an air of consummate professionalism. In fact, he understood almost nothing about the specifications and designs of ironclad warships and sailing battleships. In this field, he was practically illiterate.

All he knew in this field were aircraft carriers, air superiority, main guns, and turrets.

When he saw Cynthia silently leave and close the door behind her from the corner of his eye, Klein relaxed completely. He knew that he had finally survived the night.

Inside the master bedroom, Cynthia pursed her lips as she took the necklace from beneath her pillow and tightly clasped the finger-segment-long pendant resembling a black rhinoceros horn.

She stood there and silently prayed, Oh, great Mother Tree of Desire, please make me more alluring. Let Admiral Amyrius become even more captivated by me, so that he can derive the greatest pleasure from me and give me a child...

Inside the quiet study, Klein flipped through the pile of documents before him. Following the procedures described in the information, he selected the simple ones and signed Amyrius Rieveldt's name on them. He could imitate the admiral's signature because Faceless allowed him to remember a target's distinctive traits, while Clown gave him the precise control required.

The complicated ones he couldn't understand had notes written with the following message: "Continue studying the matter."

After "busying" himself until midnight, Klein reached out to cover his mouth and yawned slightly.

This won't do. I need to sleep. I have plenty of challenges tomorrow. I have to maintain sufficient strength and energy... Klein thought for a moment and gave up the idea of returning to the master bedroom.

He raised his right hand and pressed down on his half-closed eyes. He pulled them down and moved his eyes to his nose bridge.

Following that, Klein opened two slits where his eyes had originally been and shaped his flesh into a pair of fake eyes.

After becoming a Faceless, I'm really beginning to resemble a monster... If only I had such a godly technique back in school... He sighed silently. He half leaned his body as he kept his real eyes closed while his fake eyes were left open to "read" the documents.

His specialty as a Clown allowed him to maintain his balance. He remained motionless like a stone statue.

After an unknown period of time, Klein suddenly awoke from his dream. He sensed that there was a force attempting to pull him into a deep slumber before infiltrating him!

Who is it? I haven't done anything recently. Why would anyone enter my dream? That's not right. I'm Admiral Amyrius Rieveldt now... Klein's heart stirred as he immediately changed his appearance. Even in his dream, he assumed the form of that demigod.

Meanwhile, he felt his consciousness tightly contract as though it was avoiding something.

This is different from an ordinary dream infiltration. I've clearly regained lucidity, and I should be able to escape and wake up on my own, yet I can't leave this dreamscape... Klein noticed numerous peculiarities as he made several attempts.

He knew very well that he was sleeping, and he could sense and control his body outside the dream, but no matter what he did, he was unable to wake up!

Right on the heels of that, he saw a blurry white gas form an indiscernible figure.

Klein narrowed his eyes slightly as he tore through the dream with his psyche, allowing his real body to move silently. Then, he put his palm into his pocket to touch the Ninth Law charm.

At that moment, the blurry figure calmly said, "Do not participate in the matter regarding Aston.

"This is a warning."

... Quite impressive, daring to warn a demigod... What are Aston and the others planning? Klein thought for a moment and released his spirituality, prompting the Ninth Law charm to emanate an overwhelming air of authority. He then reproduced that aura in his dream and asked in a deep voice, "Who are you?

"Who do you represent?"

The blurry figure was taken aback before he said with a laughing sigh, "As expected of Law Weaver Amyrius. You actually managed to maintain your lucidity in such a situation."

No, no, no. Although Amyrius bears the title of Law Weaver, he isn't skilled in such matters. You should say, 'As expected of Lord Fool'... Klein lampooned inwardly and said sternly, "Answer my question."

The blurry figure chuckled.

"You don't need to know who I am.

"As a demigod, you should be clear that everything is fated, likewise for the times.

"Do not resist the fate of an era, which is also the trend of the times. This will only make you a sacrifice of history."

Fate of an era, the trend of the times, sacrifice of history... When he heard this, Klein suddenly thought of a term: Twilight Hermit Order!

However, Klein said nothing, much less uttered that name, because he would be Admiral Amyrius for the next few days.

Seeing Law Weaver Amyrius fall silent, the blurry figure said nothing more. It dissolved into wisps of white mist before vanishing from the dream.

Chapter 625: Successful First Day

Phew. What exactly are Amyrius and Aston Rieveldt plotting to have drawn a warning from the Twilight Hermit Order...? Unless something can truly alter the era and influence the course of history, this ancient and secretive organization won't even show itself or reveal its existence... In his dream, Klein stood beside a magnificent castle, thoughtfully watching the spot where the blurry figure had disappeared.

Amidst his thoughts, he suddenly snapped awake and was no longer as certain about his theory.

The scheme Admiral Amyrius and his brother are planning might not truly affect the trend of the times!

Although the Twilight Hermit Order involved itself only in important matters, Klein, as the leader of another secret organization, knew perfectly well that its members would help one another in addition to pursuing the organization's mission and ideals.

From Emperor Roselle's diary, I can tell that the Twilight Hermit Order has members from different fields, social standings, and Sequences. Some fervently believe in the original Creator, awaiting the coming of twilight and the awakening of the true god, but the rest seek only their own benefit. Under such circumstances, unless the organizer strictly enforces discipline, the members will naturally entrust one another with personal missions.

Perhaps, the matter Aston Rieveldt is planning affects a particular member of the Twilight Hermit Order who was made aware of it ahead of time. He entrusted the matter to another member and used the excuse of "the trend of the times" to warn Amyrius Rieveldt...

Following this logic, the hidden matter becomes rather interesting... For an organization at the Twilight Hermit Order's level, eliminating Law Weaver Amyrius, who is likely only Sequence 4, wouldn't be too difficult. I even suspect they can mobilize three or more angels, far surpassing the seven Churches in this respect...

Then why didn't they act directly instead of issuing a warning in advance?

Back in Backlund, if an angel were to appear, who knew whether a true god might descend. Therefore, the Twilight Hermit Order had no choice but to use a more circuitous and discreet method to deal with Duke Negan. But this is Oravi Island. There aren't even any High-Sequence Beyonders stationed here...

Did they issue the warning because the Twilight Hermit Order values secrecy and wishes to avoid exposing itself through drastic methods, or because the member who commissioned the mission doesn't want Amyrius killed? Is this admiral still useful to "him," making him unwilling to abandon Amyrius even if he might be involved in something detrimental to him?

Then, his true identity seems to be confined to certain circles...

Klein made a bold assumption, but with no way of verifying it, he could only temporarily put the matter aside and relegate it to the back of his mind.

It has nothing to do with me, since the Twilight Hermit Order is warning Admiral Amyrius... As long as I don't involve myself in anything over the next few days, I should make it through the mission. Whatever happens afterward has nothing to do with me! At my present level, I'm still far from being able to investigate the Twilight Hermit Order. I don't even have the right to probe any deeper... Klein held fast to his principles and turned his attention to the Tarot Club.

Heh, I've just noticed a problem. Other secret organizations are built around their own goals and ideals, with members helping one another on the side. Our Tarot Club is even more impressive. We only have members assigning one another missions, without any common goals or ideals... No, some members do believe we have them. For example, my dear Moon believes this organization was created to save the world... The Fool Klein gave a self-deprecating laugh before leaving the dreamscape.

He smoothly erased the fake eyes and moved his real eyes back to their original position, only to pause abruptly.

That wasn't a simple dream infiltration! Klein frowned slightly as he recalled what had happened.

That was because the power had first attempted to pull him into a deep slumber!

I was already asleep, so why would he do that? Captain once said that a Nightmare can directly view dreams, so one couldn't make such a mistake... Therefore, the visitor hadn't used a Nightmare's Beyonder powers, but something else... Did he locate me through the spirit world? Or, after learning my location, did he use the sea of collective subconscious described by Ma'am Daly to influence me directly with his Beyonder powers?

Yes... I'm more inclined to the second reason. That's the only thing that can explain why I couldn't escape my dream despite having regained lucidity. It's recorded in Emperor Roselle's diary that during his participation in the suspected Twilight Hermit Order, his entry into the gathering was based on a realistic dreamscape that encompassed the entire continent. A realistic dreamscape...

Klein nodded and chuckled silently with a sigh.

Admiral Amyrius definitely didn't expect the Twilight Hermit Order's warning.

It's only because he hired me. If it were anyone else, the stand-in would've already been exposed! That Ninth Law charm was well worth it.

City of Silver, inside the spire, in the room belonging to Chief Colin Iliad.

Derrick Berg, who had been summoned, saw the grizzled, scarred Demon Hunter once again as he felt uneasy.

After he finished his greetings, Colin sized him up and asked, "You've advanced?"

"Yes, I'm already a Sequence 7 Solar High Priest." Derrick had long reported his advancement, so there was no need to hide the matter.

Colin's light-blue eyes which had seen the vicissitudes of time moved away as he casually asked, "Do you have the subsequent potion formulas?"

The old Derrick would have answered "no" immediately, but he had since learned to think before replying.

If I say "no," advancing again without the corresponding experience will definitely make me look suspicious. But if I answer "yes," the Chief might ask me to exchange it for items that could help nurture more Sun pathway Beyonders. That would undoubtedly expose my lie... Derrick's mind raced before he answered sincerely, "No."

From his point of view, experience could be forged.

After daybreak, Klein dressed with Cynthia's help, enjoyed a sumptuous breakfast, and rode to the Oravi naval base in a carriage escorted by the admiral's bodyguards.

That morning, accompanied by rear admirals and colonels, he inspected the condition of the ships, the ordnance stores, the newly built training grounds, and the bathroom that had been renovated twenty times over the past year.

After having lunch at the navy mess hall, Klein followed the schedule and summoned all officers above the rank of major to hear their reports.

Everything followed the schedule.

Then, adapting it to the circumstances, he employed Amyrius's usual wording and added a few transitions and habitual phrases, such as "There are several points" and "Allow me to elaborate on a few more points."

It was already evening by the time the briefing ended. Accompanied by Luan, Klein left the Oravi naval base and headed to a banquet at the residence of a naval supplier.

At the banquet, they casually discussed fluctuations in the prices of goods at the port. Klein imitated Admiral Amyrius by occasionally telling an old Backlund joke, drawing warm responses and praise for his humor.

After successfully acting until the banquet ended, Klein boarded the carriage, feeling exhausted in body and mind.

I have to watch every word and action, every minute and every second. Staying truly in character for an entire day is more exhausting than fighting a pirate admiral... Klein sighed inwardly and half-closed his eyes without changing his expression.

He knew that the most difficult stage of the day wasn't over!

He still had to deal with Cynthia!

Inside the villa, Cynthia learned from the attendant who had gone out to make inquiries that Admiral Amyrius was about to return.

She quietly returned to her bedroom, took out the tiny black rhinoceros horn pendant, and used a metallic plate to scratch off tiny bits of powder, mixing it into the hot water inside a white porcelain teacup.

Chapter 626: Amyrius's Decision

Looking at the light spilling from the villa into the darkness, Klein spent several seconds mentally preparing himself before alighting from the carriage. He climbed the three-tiered steps and entered through the door, where the admiral's bodyguards and servants stood in lines on either side.

Cynthia's smile became more and more brilliant as she shifted the strands of her hair to the back of her ear, revealing her fair and slender neck. Her neckline didn't have any marks left behind by a necklace.

Seeing Secretary Luan and the admiral's bodyguards head to their rooms or move outside to take up their patrol positions, Klein pulled Cynthia into an embrace and said in a deep voice, "There's no need to prepare hot water. Give me a quiet room. I need to spend the night alone."

"..."

Cynthia used her eyes to express her puzzlement and blankness.

Klein surveyed his surroundings and softly said, "Something unexpected happened. I need to enter an extremely quiet state to recover."

As he spoke, he raised his right hand to pull at his collar, revealing a patch of pale fleshy granules.

An abnormal physical condition was the excuse Admiral Amyrius had prepared for the tramp stand-in, allowing him to avoid Cynthia's attempts at intimacy without arousing suspicion. To make it more convincing, Klein used his Faceless powers to provide "evidence."

With that said, he held the cracked rhinoceros horn pendant and turned to leave the master bedroom before entering the unaffected silent study.

As an important member of the military, Amyrius was somewhat helpless before the kingdom's upper echelons and had to overlook certain matters. For example, the telegram removing Aston from his post and the new governor-general's arrival meant the replacement had departed well in advance, yet Amyrius Rieveldt, the highest commander of the Central Sonia Sea navy, had received no prior notice. On Oravi Island, however, he still held absolute authority over Secretary Luan, the local police, and the naval base personnel. He had no need to conduct the investigation himself; he had plenty of people at his disposal.

A demigod only needed to be wary of any accidents and await news. There was no need to busy themselves!

Inside the study, as Klein heard the chaotic sound of footsteps and horrified screams, questioning, and exchanges, he couldn't help but have the cosmos and the bright, lustrous star in his mind.

What does it represent? Klein frowned and sat in the armchair behind the desk. His figure sank into the darkness brought about by the curtains.

He soon made certain connections. Due to the legends from the City of Silver and Emlyn White's description of the Sanguine's situation, he confirmed that the Moon pathway controlled a portion of the authority over reproduction and proliferation. This coincided with Cynthia's intentions and actions.

Cosmos... Mr. Door informed Emperor Roselle that the Goddess's corresponding tarot card is The Star and not The Moon. Could it be that this illusory cosmos represents "Her"? Yes, The Primordial Moon is suspected to be a particular deity, angel, or high-level devil's alternate identity. The Goddess has the honorific name of "crimson moon." Could "She" be the one passing herself off as the Primordial Moon and occupying the corresponding spot so as to control the required authority? Although Klein knew that such thoughts were sacrilegious, he couldn't help but suspect the Evernight Goddess.

But he soon rejected that conjecture. This was because, be it Vampire Ancestor Lilith or the Primordial Moon, what they projected was only "reproduction and proliferation." It didn't include the recognition of gluttony, pride, and desire. This was at odds with a number of victims earlier. The other honorific titles of the Evernight Goddess didn't include the corresponding domains.

This does coincide with the traits of the Devil pathway. A Desire Apostle is an expert in such matters... Could the one subjugating the Primordial Moon be a high-level Devil, or even the King of Devils who's known as the Dark Side of the Universe, Sequence 0 Abyss? This is very possible, but the problem with this is that it shouldn't produce the illusory cosmos and bright stars. Be it Moon or Abyss, they have nothing to do with the corresponding symbols... Klein found his conjecture problematic the more he thought over the matter.

Amidst his thoughts, he suddenly thought of a concept.

It was something important back when he was first educated in mysticism but had never received a detailed description or explanation!

That was the astral world!

Apart from the Life School of Thought and a few other organizations that believed in uncommon concepts such as the world of absolute rationality, most schools of mysticism held that existence comprised the real world, the spirit world, and the astral world.

The real world needed no explanation, and knowledge of the spirit world formed the foundation of many forms of ritualistic magic and numerous Beyonder powers. Only of the astral world did Klein know virtually nothing.

Therefore, the illusory cosmos represents the astral world. The bright star is a particular existence in the astral world? I've always suspected that the astral world is where the true gods reside. Then, this imagery which represents the Primordial Moon isn't something unacceptable... Klein didn't think further as he recalled the details of the past few days while awaiting the results of his subordinates' investigations.

Time ticked by when Klein finally heard a familiar gait approach.

Secretary Luan knocked before entering. He bowed his head and coldly reported, "Your Excellency. We've already found the Naturism Sect and captured their leader.

"It was a clue provided by Miss Cynthia's parents.

"This sect has been rather active in Oravi over the past few years. Its members refuse to wear clothes in an attempt to return to nature. Their aim is to surrender to their instincts, and they believe humans can liberate their spirituality in such a state.

"Reproduction of the next generation is a subsidiary product of this belief."

After pausing for a moment, Luan added, "Their leader is a Beyonder, a Sequence 8 Lunatic."

Lunatic? A Lunatic from the Prisoner pathway? It has something to do with the Rose School of Thought? Yes, the Rose School of Thought internally has two factions, "temperance" and "indulgence." The latter's beliefs are rather identical to the present situation... Klein was alarmed as he said in a stoic tone, "Bring him here."

Chapter 630: Timeline

The leader of the Naturism Sect was named Flight Ken, a man in his thirties. His thin face had brown sideburns, and there were evil-looking tattoos on his neck.

Luan brought him before Klein. He looked haggard, as though he had suffered the most severe mental torment.

"Your Excellency, during his capture, he repeatedly exchanged his sanity for strength and is now on the verge of a mental collapse..." Luan reported the facts without considering that Admiral Amyrius might already be able to discern the details.

That's perfect... Klein had been worried that, as a Lunatic, Flight Ken would refuse to answer questions even under mental pressure. In that case, he would have had to risk arousing suspicion by dismissing his subordinates and attempting spirit channeling.

As he looked coldly at Flight Ken, Klein's left hand gleamed gold beneath the cover of the desk.

He had switched Creeping Hunger to the Interrogator's soul!

Interrogator happened to be Sequence 7 of Admiral Amyrius's Arbiter pathway!

Two indistinct flashes of lightning appeared deep in Klein's eyes, superimposing themselves over Flight Ken's figure.

Psychic Piercing was poised for attack!

However, Klein didn't use the Beyonder power directly, as it was only at the Sequence 7 level. If he used it in front of Luan, the blond secretary would immediately see through his disguise.

Pressing Psychic Piercing against Flight Ken's Spirit Body, Klein sat like the real Admiral Amyrius and asked in a deep voice, "Do you know Cynthia?"

As he spoke, he raised his right hand and let the miniature black rhinoceros horn dangle from his palm.

"What is its use?"

The dispirited Flight Ken trembled. He felt as though a dagger hovered against his psyche, ready to pierce it at any moment.

He couldn't help but lower his head as he stammered, "Yes, I know her.

"Cynthia--Miss Cynthia wished to have a child with Your Excellency, a child with Beyonder powers. She was introduced to me by her parents.

"It's a Proliferation Necklace created with an aura bestowed by the goddess. By consuming its powder and wearing it for a prolonged period, she... she would make herself irresistible to Your Excellency..."

Klein listened in silence, only half convinced by Flight Ken's explanation. He still had his doubts.

He believed this was indeed part of Cynthia's goal. Whether of her own volition or at the urging of others, this beautiful lady truly wished to have a child with Admiral Amyrius. Her persistence even after mutating made that clear.

Although the Loen Kingdom's aristocrats disliked and even discriminated against their illegitimate children, circumstances mattered. A child who successfully inherited the father's Beyonder characteristics would still be highly valued. Ancient families privy to many secrets placed particular importance on this, and the Rieveldt family was one of them.

Furthermore, Admiral Amyrius appears stern and old-fashioned, but he cherishes those close to him. Even an illegitimate child would receive his love and be treated as important... This might be the true reason Cynthia was so eager to bear his child... Klein sighed inwardly.

His doubts about Flight Ken's answer centered on the Proliferation Necklace's true effects and the possibility that the Naturism Sect had deliberately misled Cynthia.

As he intensified the pressure of Psychic Piercing, Klein silently stared into Flight Ken's eyes until the man could bear it no longer and lowered his head again.

"What other uses does this necklace have?" Klein shook the cracked pendant.

His tone was calm, as though he knew every secret. His questions were only to obtain the final confirmation.

An indescribable pressure engulfed Flight Ken, who was already on the verge of mental collapse. He crumpled to the ground and shouted almost hysterically, "I-it can corrupt you!

"As long as Cynthia consumed the powder ground from it and sincerely chanted Mother Tree of Desire's honorific name, a-any man who has sex with her and successfully has a child would be corrupted!

"Th-that will turn you into a believer of the Mother Tree of Desire! Into one of 'Her' Blessed!"

So that's how it is... Klein instantly understood the entire story, and he was no longer puzzled by the mutation that happened that very night.

The Naturism Sect's goal is to use Cynthia and the ordinary-looking Proliferation Necklace to corrupt Admiral Amyrius, turning this important Loen military figure into a devout believer of the Mother Tree of Desire. He could then help their sect grow and provide it with protection.

The key to this method's success is how thoroughly concealed and innocuous it appears. Nothing about it immediately suggests concepts such as "powerful," "lethal," or "terrifying."

Therefore, after I rejected her three times, Cynthia was unable to discharge the corruptive power of the Proliferation Necklace's powder from her body. She gradually reached her limit, finally connected with the Mother Tree of Desire, and underwent what appeared to be a sudden mutation...

Therefore, the mutated monster couldn't have posed a real threat to Admiral Amyrius. Even my use of the Ninth Law charm easily resolved the matter. That's because this wasn't the outcome the Naturism Sect wanted. They sought to corrupt Admiral Amyrius in secret, not clash with a demigod... Klein silently looked at Secretary Luan.

Luan had also gained a rough understanding of the entire situation. He immediately lowered his head and said in a deep voice, "Your Excellency, it was our oversight.

"We only monitored Miss Cynthia and the servants here without expanding the monitoring to their friends and family.

"I'm willing to accept any punishment for this, even if you have me court-martialed."

How would the real admiral reply? Klein once again immersed himself in the character of Amyrius, experiencing his recent pain, helplessness, grief, and anger.

He maintained his seating posture and sternly said, "We shall leave this for later."

The sentence carried an unspoken meaning: "I'll decide based on your performance from now on."

Luan was taken aback for a second, seemingly finding the admiral's mild punishment unbelievable.

Klein half-closed his eyes and said in a deep voice, "My father, the late Earl Rieveldt, once taught me this.

"He said to pardon the first mistake of a subordinate.

"Luan, you, as well as the other bodyguards, should be thankful for his benevolence."

Luan's gaze flickered as he drew a silent breath. No longer quite so calm, he said earnestly, "Your benevolence is admirable.

"I pledge you my utmost loyalty, second only to my principles, my god, and my kingdom."

Your pride truly knows no bounds... Klein didn't believe him and said with apparent calm, "The saying has a second half: 'Punish them for their second mistake.'"

Luan nodded, unsurprised.

Klein turned his gaze back to Flight Ken, who was slumped on the ground. Without changing his expression, he pressed, "Who instigated you to do this?"

In his view, Flight Ken would likely answer that they had devised the plan themselves. After coming into contact with Cynthia, learning of her desire, and discovering her lover's identity, the Naturism Sect had boldly conceived the idea of corrupting Admiral Amyrius.

Flight glanced left and right nervously before saying with trepidation, "I-it was the Mother Tree of Desire. 'She' instructed me to do it in a dream. I-in the beginning, I only wanted to lend Cynthia the Proliferation Necklace for her to wear. I never planned for her to consume the powder."

Instigated by the Mother Tree of Desire? This is almost equivalent to a revelation... Klein nearly frowned.

Although Admiral Amyrius is an important figure in the Loen military and the highest commander of the Central Sonia Sea, giving him considerable status and power, he shouldn't be important enough to attract an evil goddess's personal attention... However, I'm not an evil god. I have no way to comprehend how they think. Perhaps corrupting Amyrius is part of some later plan... Yes, I also can't rule out the possibility that Flight Ken is lying. I'll have to confirm it later... Klein thought before asking sternly, "When did you have this dream?"

Flight forced a fearful smile and said, "I-it was the night of 4th February, last Friday. I-I remember it very clearly. I had just released a lady's spirituality."

4th February... Klein ruminated over the date, momentarily unable to find anything special about it.

After two seconds of silence, he turned to ask, "What relationship do you have with the Rose School of Thought?

"What relationship does the Mother Tree of Desire have with the Chained God?"

He wasn't concerned that Admiral Amyrius might already know the answer or that Secretary Luan might understand the matter to some extent. The question could easily be interpreted as a request for confirmation.

Flight Ken revealed a fervor he could barely contain.

"W-we're a branch of the Rose School of Thought. My mentor is the Rose School of Thought's saint, Zatwen.

"The Chained God is another manifestation of the Mother Tree of Desire.

"The Mother Tree of Desire is the one true deity, surpassing the seven gods and the True Creator!

"Your Excellency, you are a candidate to be 'Her' Blessed!"

Anyone can boast... Does this mean the Mother Tree of Desire is another identity of the Chained God? According to the evil spirit in the underground ruins suspected to be Red Angel Medici, the Prisoner and Devil pathways are interchangeable at the divine level. Therefore, it isn't surprising that the Rose School of Thought's Chained God can magnify the various desires of living beings. The School also has internal temperance and indulgence factions... This matches the situation... Klein thought as he turned to Luan and said, "Leave us for a while."

He planned to question Flight Ken about whether he had committed any evil deeds and whether he was suitable as a meal for Creeping Hunger.

"Yes, Your Excellency." Without asking why, Luan briskly walked out of the study and closed the door behind him.

After the echo of the closing door faded, the entire study suddenly fell silent, as though isolated from the outside world.

A Baron of Corruption's Beyonder power? Did he Distort the act of "closing the door" to seal the study? Very considerate... Klein pondered for a few seconds as he recalled his earlier train of thought. Then he suddenly asked, "6th February. What did you do on Sunday evening?"

Flight Ken was taken aback as he said, "W-we followed the revelation given by Mother Tree of Desire, and using the Proliferation Necklace and a hair obtained from a particular tramp, we held a ritual to magnify the tramp's gluttony."

You really did kill the tramp meant to serve as Admiral Amyrius's stand-in! Was it to prevent the admiral from leaving, thereby making him fall into the trap, have sex with Cynthia, and become corrupted? Klein instantly connected all the pieces.

And my appearance introduced another possibility that happened to ruin the plan... Coincidence... That's not right! When the word "coincidence" flashed through his mind, Klein suddenly recalled what was peculiar about 4th February, the day the Mother Tree of Desire sent Flight Ken a revelation in a dream.

It was the first day of his arrival on Oravi Island!

Chapter 631: Three Days of Absence

Don't tell me this whole affair was aimed at me? The thought jolted Klein fully awake.

After several similar experiences, he had developed something of a persecution complex.

It really is possible... Just as I arrived on Oravi Island, the Mother Tree of Desire sent Flight Ken a revelation through a dream and set an entire series of plans in motion. The day I met Bilt Brando was also the day the Naturism Sect held the ritual that caused the tramp serving as a stand-in to die of gluttony...

If they were targeting Admiral Amyrius, the plan needed to ensure that a stand-in was available and that he couldn't find another helper or some other means of concealing his departure. But clearly, this couldn't be guaranteed. My involvement is proof...

If I was the target, that explains the previous problem. Klein used his Clown powers to control his expression as he regarded Flight Ken somberly.

With such a theory, more questions emerged in his mind. But how were they certain that Bilt Brando would seek me out?

The news that Gehrman Sparrow could change into anyone came from Vice Admiral Ailment Tracy. That was a factor beyond the Mother Tree of Desire's control...

Of course, from another perspective, it was precisely the spread of this news, combined with my arrival on Oravi Island, that prompted the Mother Tree of Desire to send the revelation and put the plan into motion.

But why would "She" want to corrupt me? My grudge against the Rose School of Thought isn't serious enough to attract the attention of an evil god. All I did was work with Miss Sharron and Maric to kill a Sequence 5 Wraith, a Sequence 6 Zombie, and a Sequence 7 Werewolf, taking the Scarlet Lunar Corona and Biological Poison Bottle... My hunt for the Admiral of Blood ended before it even began. It was merely an idea that was never put into action. The only thing that actually happened was the killing of Steel Maveti...

The act of revenge that resulted from this matter shouldn't even exceed the attention of a saint!

Was there something special about me that resonated with an item formed from the Mother Tree of Desire's aura the moment I set foot on this island?

But I've never experienced any abnormal reactions from the holy artifacts left behind by other deities in Backlund or Tingen City.

Furthermore, I divined the matter above the gray fog and received a revelation that the tramp's death was purely a coincidence. It wasn't arranged by a mythical creature or a Grade 0 Sealed Artifact, yet the outcome proved otherwise...

Th-this is the first time that divination above the gray fog has been disrupted in the true sense of the word? I didn't even notice that it had been disrupted?

Klein's thoughts paused as he discovered the most serious problem.

Back when the matter involved 0-08, all he received was an ineffective revelation and not a disrupted outcome!

Therefore, was it a power exceeding that of a Grade 0 Sealed Artifact? The Mother Tree of Desire personally interfered, and "She" is a true deity "Herself"! But it's very difficult for the seven gods to influence the real world. There has to be a corresponding ritual... Yes, I also divined the risks of acting as Amyrius above the gray fog and received a revelation that the risk was acceptable. The outcome does match how the situation developed, so that divination wasn't disrupted? The more Klein thought about it, the more perplexed he became.

What he found most incomprehensible was that, if he was the target of the corruption, why hadn't he faced any difficult trials? Why had he instead resolved the problem relatively easily?

This made the Mother Tree of Desire's arrangement seem like a joke! With these concerns weighing on his mind, Klein nearly crushed Flight Ken. He questioned him again to verify his conjecture.

To his surprise, the Naturism Sect hadn't held any rituals over the past week to request that the Mother Tree of Desire disrupt divination. Nor had they made any special arrangements intended to make the plan fail.

Strange... Klein took out a gold coin and flicked it as a final confirmation.

Even without divination, he was almost certain that Flight Ken wasn't lying. First, the man was already on the brink of mental collapse and lacked the presence of mind to fabricate a plausible story. Second, Flight Ken's answers were logical and fully corroborated everything Klein had deliberately withheld.

The gold coin landed in his open palm, the king's portrait facing up, indicating a positive response.

Combined with the divination statement, Klein finally confirmed that Flight Ken wasn't lying.

Temporarily suppressing his puzzlement, he looked at Flight Ken once more and asked without a smile, "What have you done in the past that violated the kingdom's laws or society's moral principles?"

Flight Ken was taken aback for a few seconds, and his mental state seemed to improve instantly.

From his point of view, his plot against Admiral Amyrius was his most serious crime and the one most likely to enrage this important figure. Everything else was trivial, nothing that required a demigod to waste his time and energy on.

Therefore, setting aside the important matter and moving on to an ordinary topic meant that he might receive a favorable outcome.

Flight Ken couldn't help but reveal a faint smile as he hastened to recount his deeds.

"I once tortured a family for an entire night for their property, murdered them, and abandoned their bodies in the woods. Then, I used forged documents to obtain a sizable amount of wealth.

"I deliberately enticed many believers to indulge in their desires and watched as their emptiness and regret after the deed slowly paved the way for them to become fertilizer for the Mother Tree of Desire.

"I once tricked many ladies into following their nature, claiming that the spirits would redeem them, and used that as an excuse to possess them.

"I tortured believers who attempted to renounce the religion, slicing off every part that protruded from their bodies..."

He recounted every one of his sins without any thought of concealing the truth.

Klein could scarcely believe what he heard. He had never imagined anyone could be so evil.

As Flight Ken grew increasingly excited while recounting his past deeds, he saw Admiral Amyrius stand expressionlessly, walk around the desk, and stop in front of him. The admiral then raised his left palm.

A terrifying mouth appeared in the center of the left palm, revealing two rows of illusory, eerie white teeth that were cold as ice.

"No... No!"

A shrill, terrified scream echoed in the room for a long time until silence prevailed.

After a while, Klein bent down to pick up a blob of grayish-white light that resembled a shrunken brain.

This was the Beyonder characteristic of a Lunatic!

Unfortunately, Flight Ken had been searched before being brought here. He didn't leave behind any cash or items.

Klein snapped his fingers and ignited the rest of the clothes.

Watching his left shoulder begin to recover, he sat behind the desk and remained silent amid the dancing scarlet flames.

Once the traces were no longer noticeable, he pulled at a corresponding rope, making the bell outside ring.

Luan released the restriction and entered, instinctively observing every corner.

"Instruct the guards and base personnel to cooperate with the Oravi police in purging the Naturism Sect. It would be best if they could find some clues and capture several members of the Rose School of Thought," Klein ordered solemnly.

"Yes, Your Excellency," Luan answered respectfully.

He didn't ask where Flight Ken was, as though the leader of the Naturism Sect never existed.

Klein didn't pay too much attention to the raid on Oravi Island. Using the excuse that he was slightly injured, he declined the remaining schedules for the next two days.

This meant that, after expending the Ninth Law charm, he wouldn't need to worry about projecting the might of a High-Sequence Beyonder of the Arbiter pathway.

Near noon, Luan knocked and entered before reporting, "Your Excellency, Mr. Aston has yet to depart on the liner. The new Governor-General, Ben Conrad, has invited you to a banquet he's hosting tonight."

Ben Conrad... The person who replaced Aston as governor-general... According to what I know, Viscount Conrad's family is a loyal subject of the royal family. Talim likely got to know Prince Edessak while being the equestrian teacher of Viscount Conrad's youngest son... Klein nodded gently as he immersed himself in Amyrius Rieveldt's complex emotions.

He remained silent for a few seconds before saying, "Inform Governor-General Conrad that it's inconvenient for me to attend the banquet because of the injuries I suffered in the Naturism Sect's assault.

"Apologize to him on my behalf."

"Yes, Your Excellency." Luan didn't persuade him otherwise and calmly left the room.

A demigod definitely had the right to be "willful!"

As long as he didn't engage in some secret ploy or commit any serious mistake, there was no way he would be blamed regardless of what he did. This was because any faction would be willing to rope in a demigod!

As he watched Luan disappear through the door, Klein shook his head almost imperceptibly and sat back down behind the desk.

A quiet day quickly passed. When it was almost midnight, Klein, who was sleeping in the guest room, suddenly woke up and sat up.

His spiritual perception told him that someone had entered the house!

Klein focused his gaze on the window and saw a middle-aged man in a tailcoat. He had black hair and blue eyes, and the corners of his mouth sagged slightly. He was none other than Admiral Amyrius!

Phew! He's finally back... Klein slowly stood up and cautiously asked, "What item did you give me before?"

He was afraid that the Admiral Amyrius before him was also a Faceless in disguise.

Admiral Amyrius stood there and answered with a stern expression, "The Ninth Law charm."

Without waiting for Gehrman Sparrow to speak again, he took two steps forward and calmly asked, "Did anything happen in the past few days?"

Klein controlled his facial muscles and said, "Your secretary committed a mistake and was injured."

"Oh? What else?" Admiral Amyrius nodded with grave reserve.

Klein tried hard not to move his gaze away as he looked straight at him and said, "Your younger brother has been relieved of his post as governor-general. The new governor-general has already taken over.

"Your Miss Cynthia mutated into a monster and was purified by me.

"Several of your bodyguards and attendants were infected to varying degrees and are currently receiving treatment."

Shock gradually appeared on Amyrius's face despite his efforts to control his expression.

His eyes moved slightly, as though he suspected whether he had returned to the wrong place.

It had only been three days!

Chapter 632: Finishing Up

After a frozen moment of silence, Amyrius quickly brought his expression under control. In a deep, commanding tone, he said, "Tell me the specifics."

Klein didn't hide much of the truth. He began with Aston Rieveldt's question, before mentioning the warning from the mysterious person in the dream, and how he was told not to make a choice that resisted the trend of the times. Following that, he talked about the sudden telegram, and finally he spoke of Cynthia's mutation. He went into detail about the situation, the arrogance of the secretary, how he dealt with it, and the general investigations that followed.

The only information he kept to himself was that February 4th had been his first day on Oravi Island, as well as his subsequent guesses.

Of course, he was also very vague about the combat. He believed that Admiral Amyrius would understand. After all, a Beyonder's specialties and unique abilities were among their greatest secrets. If others learned of them, they could set up a targeted ambush, making it possible for a Beyonder to die at the hands of an enemy of a lower Sequence.

Below the level of High-Sequence Beyonders, many Beyonders could be very powerful or very weak!

Amyrius listened to the account in silence, his expression seemingly unchanged in the dark room. However, the fact that he hadn't interrupted Klein implied something.

After a few seconds of silence, he asked in a deep voice, "The mysterious person who infiltrated your dream emphasized the fate of an era, as well as the trend of the times?"

"Yes." Klein had already changed back into Gehrman Sparrow's appearance. He didn't deliberately go into detail that the mysterious person had never mentioned Aston Rieveldt's exact plan.

Amyrius fell silent again.

"You maintained my appearance in your dream?"

"Yes, this is my secret," Klein answered succinctly.

Amyrius nodded almost imperceptibly as he paced within a small area before looking back at Gehrman Sparrow.

"There was nothing wrong with your choices."

Upon hearing this, Klein seemed to receive the ultimate and most effective feedback. A large portion of the potion in him was digested as a result.

In a deep, reverberating voice, Amyrius turned to the side and said without any hint of emotion, "I originally had no intention of signing a contract with the stand-in. Instead, I planned to tell Cynthia that I was showing symptoms of losing control, making it impossible for me to have physical relations with her for five days. However, I later changed my mind for certain reasons."

If not for the temporary contract's forceful restriction, even if I'm able to withstand the temptations during usual times, I might not have been able to resist the magnified desires last night...

Of course, if Cynthia had known about the five-day restriction in advance, she wouldn't have been in such a hurry to consume the powder ground from the Proliferation Necklace. That would've prevented her from sensing the Mother Tree of Desire and ultimately mutating... But in that case, the initiative would've rested with the Naturism Sect. They could've invented an excuse to make Cynthia consume it...

This matter really was aimed at me... What does the Mother Tree of Desire actually want? Also, what caused Admiral Amyrius to make such an important change...? Klein seemed to grasp the crux of the matter as he looked at Amyrius's profile.

"What's the reason?"

Amyrius said with a deadpan expression, "There's no need for you to know."

For the first time in his life, Klein hated such a response.

After some thought, he took out the cracked pendant and threw it at Amyrius.

"This is the Proliferation Necklace that originated from the Mother Tree of Desire."

Amyrius raised his hand to catch it before looking down.

"You may leave.

"Go to Bilt to receive your reward."

Aren't you worried that I know of something I'm not supposed to know? That's right. Aston's ploy has been exposed and is known by his political enemies. As for Amyrius, he was clearly not involved; therefore, it doesn't matter if I understand anything... Klein imagined that there would be a long-term confidential contract, but to his surprise, he could leave just like that.

Noticing that Amyrius didn't raise another matter, he pointed at him and said, "Clothes."

Only then did Admiral Amyrius look up, silently taking off the formal attire consisting of a shirt and tailcoat.

Klein had originally intended to ask for additional payment because the excessive danger had gone beyond the scope of the original agreement. However, after secretly confirming that he was responsible for Cynthia's fate, he guiltily abandoned the idea. He quickly took off the comfortable sleeping robe, changed into a tailcoat, and, under Amyrius's guidance, leaped out the window and secretly left the garden villa while the bodyguards' eyes were "confounded."

Amyrius donned a sleeping robe and clenched the Proliferation Necklace before walking to the window. He stood there silently as he faced the crimson moon and few stars in the dark night.

He stood there expressionlessly, remaining in the same posture for a long time.

Pa!

He slightly relaxed his clenched fist, and fragments of the miniature rhinoceros horn fell to the ground one after another.

The next day at noon, Klein, who had slept soundly the entire night, left the inn and took a carriage to Sweet Lemon Bar. He went to the second floor to meet Bilt Brando.

"It's over?" Bilt asked with relief and surprise.

Klein nodded and said, "Admiral Amyrius has returned.

"Where's the rest of the payment?"

Bilt revealed an unconcealable smile. After he dismissed his guards, he personally went to a safe and took out the remaining 500 pounds and a blue cufflink.

"This is the mystical item created from the murloc bladder you provided," Bilt explained. "It can produce illusory scales beneath your skin, making you as difficult to catch as a fish. It will also help you withstand a certain amount of damage. With it, you can dive to a depth of at least fifteen meters without any protective gear and move freely underwater for ten minutes. It doesn't have any serious negative effects. It will merely make you tire easily in hot or arid places."

That means this cufflink shouldn't be used with the Sun Brooch... Klein extended his hand to collect the money and item.

"Aren't you afraid that there will be problems?" Bilt cracked a joke.

"Your business is here," Klein answered calmly.

His meaning was clear. If there were any problems, he wasn't afraid that the person in charge would go missing.

Bilt's smile froze as he said, "It hasn't been named. You can give it one."

"Murloc Cufflink." Klein couldn't be bothered to waste his brain cells.

"Nice name. It leaves quite an impression," Bilt said with a forced smile.

He paused for a moment before saying, "That Artisan said he can inscribe a spirit world creature's summoning ritual onto a spiritually rich material. It will last for at most a year and a half, but you'll need to provide detailed information.

"Heh heh, according to our agreement, I'll be responsible for it, so there's nothing to be worried about."

"Alright. Make it a harmonica." Klein took out a pen and paper from his pocket and scribbled the ritual to summon his messenger.

"Messenger? This is rather rare. It's very hard for Beyonders not from the Death pathway to find a suitable one." Bilt received the piece of paper and casually scanned it. Chapter 633: Two Types of Parasitizing

After leaving the Sweet Lemon Bar, Klein returned straight to his inn.

Just as he was packing his luggage, the gigantic skeleton messenger suddenly appeared.

It had intended to pat Klein on the shoulder, but its nearly four-meter-tall body made that impossible, with its head passing through the ceiling. All it could do was toss the letter forward before collapsing into nothingness.

It's very difficult for me to describe such matters accurately. Isn't the latter situation somewhat similar to Little Sun's? He prayed to me without much thought, and I discovered the existence of Amon's avatar. This might indicate the first form of parasitism, but the problem is that several months have passed. It's possible that the Parasite has taken complete control of Leonard.

I have to first confirm the matter before knowing what I should do. Otherwise, rashly entering his dream will only lead to preemptively warning the Parasite, making the problem more serious and harder to resolve...

If it's the second state, my dear poet believes in the Goddess, but "She" has millions of believers. It's unlikely that "She" would pay special attention to a Red Glove who's neither a Blessed nor a saint...

She's unlike me, The Fool. I read every prayer and often provide a one-to-one service.

This is what it's like during the "startup phase"...

As Klein thought over it, he suddenly sighed and felt wistful.

He had originally planned to entrust Emlyn with secretly monitoring Leonard Mitchell to see whether he often whispered to himself. Therefore, Klein rationally abandoned the idea.

Miss Magician is only a Sequence 8 Trickmaster. She isn't capable of monitoring an angel-level Parasite or a Red Glove...

Miss Justice's identity is the best form of concealment. Besides, she's also a believer of the Goddess. But the problem lies in the fact that she's back in her fief. She will only return to Backlund in June...

Mr. Hanged Man and Ma'am Hermit are at sea. As for Little Sun, there's no way for him to connect with the outside world...

There still aren't enough Tarot Club members, and the networks they've developed remain inadequate. I can't find anyone suitable to help me complete this task.

Among the people I know, Miss Sharron would be the best choice for such a task when it comes to concealment, but I have no way to contact her, even through Miss Magician or Emlyn... Sigh, I fled Backlund in such a hurry that I failed to consider many of the consequences...

Klein raised his hand to knead his temples as he thought about how Leonard was an elite Red Glove in the Nighthawks. Perhaps I can wait until I digest the potion and head to the eastern reaches of the Sonia Sea to find mermaids. After completing my advancement, I can return to Backlund and do it myself... The experienced Klein hesitated no further and quickly made up his mind.

The weather in East Chester County during April was comfortable. The vegetation was lush and the scenery was beautiful. It was the perfect season for hunting.

Audrey wore a fitted black riding suit and a helmet. Mounted on her burgundy mare, she pursued a brightly colored wild chicken.

She shot a whistling arrow and accurately struck the prey.

As a Psychiatrist, her physical attributes had clearly been enhanced. Combined with the archery training she had received from a young age, this made her quite skilled with both firearms and bows.

A golden figure pounced forward and rapidly bit onto the prey which had lost its life. It was none other than Susie.

"It's a pleasure working with you." Audrey smiled and tapped her palm against Susie's paw.

At that moment, the aristocrats surrounding her came over, either praising her for her hunting prowess or her training of her hunting hound.

Audrey felt a little ashamed regarding the latter half.

She had never trained Susie before!

The aristocrats quickly dispersed and resumed chasing their prey. The low-ranking priest of the Evernight Goddess approached and said to Audrey in a hushed voice, "You can receive the Hypnotist formula after completing the final mission."

Finally... Audrey's eyes lit up as she silently nodded.

To be frank, if she were still the green and squeamish noble lady of the past, she would've long lost the patience to accumulate the required contributions and would've ended up requesting the purchase of the Hypnotist potion formula at the Tarot Club.

But she knew very well that accumulating contributions would aid her infiltration of the Psychology Alchemists. She would gain more trust and lay a solid foundation for obtaining High-Sequence potion formulas in the future. Therefore, she patiently completed every task, whether interesting or not.

Of course, Audrey hadn't wasted the past two months. She had eavesdropped on conversations among the aristocrats and maidservants, guided them into pouring out their frustrations, and helped them overcome their negative emotions. This allowed her to act as a Psychiatrist to near perfection.

During this process, she realized that she would unconsciously eavesdrop on, observe, and steer the people around her to understand their flaws and habitual thought processes. She believed she could place any one of them at a disadvantage or mentally break them without leaving a trace. She could also make them help her of their own volition without even realizing it.

I have to admit, it's rather terrifying, like the monsters in legends that can seize control of your mind... Recalling the experience, Audrey couldn't help sighing inwardly.

Smine was somewhat unaccustomed to riding as she carefully circled the area and said, "Stoen University's Associate Professor Michele is a collector. We want you to purchase a notebook from the Twenty Year War."

The Twenty Year War was fought between the Loen Kingdom and the Feysac Empire from 621 to 642 of the Fifth Epoch. The former was defeated and lost the ancient elven island that is now Sonia Island.

Chapter 634: City of White

Visiting Associate Professor Michele, viewing his collection, and then asking to buy one of its items shouldn't be too difficult.

On the tumultuous sea, the Blue Avenger was like a leaf tumbling amid heavy squalls. From time to time, it would be hurled upward before crashing back into the waves, but it maintained a stunning balance without any sign of capsizing.

Inside the captain's cabin, Alger Wilson hovered by the window, watching the mountain-high waves outside. Fierce winds silently swirled around him.

Some time later, he planted his feet on the carpet.

Wind-blessed is indeed a Sequence whose name lends itself to "acting." The only problem is that it occasionally makes me irascible, perfectly matching the temperament of a gale... Alger sighed inwardly, feeling no delight.

Over the past two months or so, he had failed to obtain the Ocean Songster formula through his own channels or trades in the Tarot Club. After all, it was a Sequence 5 formula, the Sequence closest to a demigod. Demand for such a formula far outstripped supply. Even with boatloads of money and a willingness to pay a premium, it was very difficult to buy one.

Normally, joining the corresponding Church or organization was the most effective way to obtain a formula, but as a bishop of the Church of Storms, Alger couldn't rely on this method. Due to certain secrets, he needed to conceal his strength to gain the freedom to act while remaining under only light surveillance. Only after he had sufficient confidence would he head to a particular place and complete the goal he had pursued all this time.

To his relief, he hadn't wasted the past two months. At the very least, he had digested the Wind-blessed potion at a fairly good pace.

The Wind-blessed is simple. Ocean Songster is said to be quite difficult... Must I sing frequently? Alger couldn't help turning his head toward the deck.

Although he was separated from them by several rooms, he could still hear the drunken sailors singing with gusto, creating a din that could rival the storm's roars.

Alger unknowingly frowned.

Backlund, Cherwood Borough. Synthes Circus.

"Ma'am, didn't you say you perform magic? Why are you dressed like that?" a puzzled youth asked. The woman wore a pitch-black pointed hat and a dress of the same color.

I don't know why I'm wearing this either. I must've lost my mind from the cold when I first appeared here, and the costume became permanent afterward... Fors rubbed her face, which was painted red and yellow, and replied with a smile, "In ancient times, magic was often mistaken for witchcraft."

But this has nothing to do with why I'm wearing this... She picked up the middle of the three porcelain cups in front of her, then placed a white ball beneath it.

It was a dove!

As for the tiny ball from before, it was now where the dove had been!

Although being a Trickmaster feels very nice, that won't stop me from pursuing my goal of advancing to Astrologer. Teacher said he would give me the formula, ingredients, and a gift this week... What kind of gift will it be?

Fors raised her right hand to cover her mouth and yawned languidly. Then, she said with a smile, "Well, I'm actually a best-selling author. My next book is about circuses, so I came here to work."
